
===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_production_fields.py =====
"""Opt-in metadata for a NEW frozen inspiration prompt, never a scoring rule.

Old contracts are untouched. Creating a candidate requires explicitly applying
this extension to its prompt output contract and adding the matching guidance.
"""
from copy import deepcopy

METADATA_FIELDS = ("class_text", "style", "tags", "cons", "design")
GUIDANCE = """
【正式业务字段：与前述评测事实一起输出】
只描述当前待评图片的可见内容，不描述参照锚点，不读取或猜测人工等级。
title、seotitle 沿用现有标题规则。额外完整输出以下字段：
- class_text：图片主体的业务分类文字，保持与 primary_category、secondary_category 一致。
- style：可见的设计风格；证据不足时写“风格不明确”，禁止编造品牌、作者或项目。
- tags：字符串数组，列出与主体、空间、风格、材质等直接相关、无重复的可检索标签；无法识别时返回空数组，不凑数。
- cons：图片中可见的不足说明，无明显不足时明确写“未见明显不足”。不能因为用于推荐而隐去缺陷。
- design：简洁说明构图、空间、材质、色彩或设计亮点，只陈述可见事实，不编造地点、尺寸、品牌和作者。
这些字段用于运营核验与发布，不参与总分或等级计算，不得据此修改红线、缺陷、赛道、媒介或美感结论。
reason、trait、image_defects 沿用原合同的类型和取值，最终 score、grade 仍由平台冻结规则确定。
""".strip()


def extend_contract(contract):
    from .prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract
    result = deepcopy(contract)
    parse_output_contract(result)
    schema = result["schema"]
    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
    if (schema.get("type") != "object" or schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False
            or not {"title", "seotitle", "reason", "trait", "image_defects", "track_classification"} <= set(properties)
            or set(METADATA_FIELDS) & set(properties)):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_production_source_contract_invalid")
    text = {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}
    for field in METADATA_FIELDS:
        properties[field] = ({"type": "array", "items": deepcopy(text)}
                             if field == "tags" else deepcopy(text))
    schema["required"] = [*schema["required"], *METADATA_FIELDS]
    parse_output_contract(result)
    return result


def published_fields(raw, *, draft=False):
    """Render frozen A arrays as protocol text without inventing absent values."""
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_invalid")
    fields = {}
    for name in ("title", "seo_title", "class_text", "style", "tags", "cons",
                 "design", "reason", "trait", "image_defects"):
        source = name if name in raw else {"seo_title": "seotitle", "class_text": "category"}.get(name)
        if source is None or source not in raw:
            if draft:
                fields[name] = [] if name == "tags" else ""
                continue
            raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_missing")
        value = deepcopy(raw[source])
        if name in {"reason", "image_defects"} and isinstance(value, list):
            if any(not isinstance(v, str) or not v.strip() for v in value):
                if draft:
                    fields[name] = ""
                    continue
                raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_invalid")
            value = "；".join(value)
        if name == "tags":
            if not isinstance(value, list) or any(not isinstance(v, str) or not v.strip() for v in value):
                if draft:
                    fields[name] = []
                    continue
                raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_invalid")
        elif not isinstance(value, str):
            if draft:
                fields[name] = ""
                continue
            raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_invalid")
        if not draft and name in {"title", "seo_title", "class_text", "style", "cons", "design"} and not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("inspiration_projection_fields_empty")
        fields[name] = value
    return fields


def class_text_from_categories(raw):
    """下游 class_text 固定为「一级-二级」（老大 09-28 定）。

    一级为空 → 其它-其它（不管二级）；二级为空 → 一级-其它；两者相同照样写「一级-二级」。
    分类优先读 A 段顶层；顶层两个都没有时再读 ``production_fields``（评审 T2：模型把分类放进
    production_fields 时，不能悄悄全变成兜底的「其它-其它」）。两处整体二选一，不混拼。
    """
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return "其它-其它"

    def _has_category(source):
        return any(isinstance(source.get(key), str) and source.get(key).strip()
                   for key in ("primary_category", "secondary_category"))

    nested = raw.get("production_fields")
    if not _has_category(raw) and isinstance(nested, dict) and _has_category(nested):
        raw = nested
    primary = raw.get("primary_category")
    secondary = raw.get("secondary_category")
    primary = primary.strip() if isinstance(primary, str) else ""
    secondary = secondary.strip() if isinstance(secondary, str) else ""
    if not primary:
        return "其它-其它"
    return f"{primary}-{secondary or '其它'}"

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_quality_metrics.py =====
"""Five-level and three-bucket quality metrics for inspiration images."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
BUCKETS = {
    "recommendation": frozenset({"L1", "L2"}),
    "ordinary": frozenset({"L3", "L4"}),
    "filter": frozenset({"L5"}),
}


def _level(value: Any) -> str | None:
    normalized = str(value or "").strip().upper()
    return normalized if normalized in LEVELS else None


def _binary(rows: list[tuple[str, str]], positive: set[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    predicted = sum(pred in positive for _truth, pred in rows)
    actual = sum(truth in positive for truth, _pred in rows)
    true_positive = sum(truth in positive and pred in positive for truth, pred in rows)
    false_positive = predicted - true_positive
    false_negative = actual - true_positive
    precision = true_positive / predicted if predicted else 0.0
    recall = true_positive / actual if actual else 0.0
    return {
        "support": len(rows),
        "denominator": actual,
        "predicted_count": predicted,
        "actual_count": actual,
        "tp": true_positive,
        "fp": false_positive,
        "fn": false_negative,
        "precision": precision,
        "recall": recall,
    }


def three_bucket_fallback_metrics(rows: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    normalized = [
        (truth, pred)
        for row in rows
        if (truth := _level(row.get("truth"))) is not None
        and (pred := _level(row.get("pred"))) is not None
    ]
    output: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for bucket, members in BUCKETS.items():
        output[bucket] = _binary(
            normalized,
            set(members),
        )
    return output


def compute_inspiration_quality_metrics(rows: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized = [
        (truth, pred)
        for row in rows
        if (truth := _level(row.get("truth"))) is not None
        and (pred := _level(row.get("pred"))) is not None
    ]
    per_level: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    confusion = Counter(normalized)
    for level in LEVELS:
        per_level[level] = _binary(normalized, {level})
    recommendation = _binary(normalized, set(BUCKETS["recommendation"]))
    recommendation["share"] = (
        sum(pred in BUCKETS["recommendation"] for _truth, pred in normalized) / len(normalized)
        if normalized
        else 0.0
    )
    return {
        "total": len(normalized),
        "per_level": per_level,
        "recommendation": recommendation,
        "three_bucket": three_bucket_fallback_metrics(
            [{"truth": truth, "pred": pred} for truth, pred in normalized]
        ),
        "confusion": {f"{truth}->{pred}": count for (truth, pred), count in sorted(confusion.items())},
        "diagnostics": {
            "l1_overpromotion_cost": per_level["L1"]["fp"],
            "l5_missed_filter_count": per_level["L5"]["fn"],
        },
    }


def quality_gate(metrics: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    failures: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    recommendation = metrics.get("recommendation") or {}
    if float(recommendation.get("share", 0.0)) > 0.35:
        failures.append({"gate": "recommendation_share", "value": recommendation.get("share"), "limit": 0.35})
    checks = (("recommendation", recommendation), ("L3", (metrics.get("per_level") or {}).get("L3", {})), ("L4", (metrics.get("per_level") or {}).get("L4", {})), ("L5", (metrics.get("per_level") or {}).get("L5", {})))
    for name, values in checks:
        for metric in ("precision", "recall"):
            if float(values.get(metric, 0.0)) < 0.80:
                failures.append({"gate": f"{name}_{metric}", "value": values.get(metric, 0.0), "limit": 0.80})
    return failures


===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_quality_rules.py =====
"""灵感图质量规则机制（可配置版）。

本模块只负责两件事，且必须只负责这两件事：

1. 随手拍限分（snapshot_limit）——识别到"随手拍"一类信号时，把总分压到某个上限。
2. 硬伤例外名单（defect_exceptions）——满足佐证条件时，让某个硬伤不触发降级。

设计要点
--------
运营用**人话字段**配置（"当判定理由包含…"、"最高只能得几分"），装载器负责翻译成
执行层 ``inspiration_aesthetic_foundation`` 已经在消费的内部形状。因此执行层的
评分逻辑一行不改，风险面只落在翻译层。

刻意不复用 ``_validated_quality_rules``
---------------------------------------
旧校验把内容写死了：``match_any`` 必须精确等于 ``["是随手拍"]``、豁免必须恰好 1 条、
``key`` 必须叫 ``subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark``、关键词必须精确等于
``["品牌文字", "品牌字样"]``。运营改任何一处都会被拒——那正是本模块要解开的锁。
所以这里自带装载器，只做结构与取值域校验，不锁定业务内容。

单一职责（切记）
----------------
本机制**只管上面两项**。锚点图片归 ``inspiration_anchor_mechanism``、分档切点归合同
顶层 ``level_thresholds`` / ``level_scale``、八维定义归 ``dimensions``、红线归
``redline_policy``。任何外来机制键混进本块都会被 ``assert_quality_rules_isolated``
拦下——混进来会让调试失去可归因性，``inspiration_aesthetic_foundation`` 就是把锚图、
阈值、维度、封顶揉在一起的反面样本。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from typing import Any

CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY = "quality_rules"

# 本块允许出现的顶层字段——白名单，杜绝改个名字夹带外来机制。
_ALLOWED_BLOCK_FIELDS = frozenset({
    "enabled",
    "snapshot_limit",
    "defect_exceptions",
    "notes",
})

_ALLOWED_SNAPSHOT_FIELDS = frozenset({
    "enabled",
    "name",
    "signal",
    "when_reason_contains",
    "max_score",
    "max_level",
    "dimension_ceilings",
})

_ALLOWED_EXCEPTION_FIELDS = frozenset({
    "name",
    "defect",
    "defect_source",
    "when_evidence_contains",
    "require_dimensions",
})

_ALLOWED_REQUIREMENT_FIELDS = frozenset({
    "dimension",
    "min_grade",
    "no_shortcomings",
    "no_deduction_hits",
    "max_deduction",
})

# 不属于本机制的键——一旦出现就报错，并直接告诉它该去哪。
_FOREIGN_KEYS: dict[str, str] = {
    "score_thresholds": "应放在合同顶层 level_thresholds",
    "level_thresholds": "应放在合同顶层，不属于质量规则块",
    "level_scale": "应放在合同顶层 level_scale",
    "bands": "分档切点应放在合同顶层 level_thresholds",
    "anchors": "锚点图片应放在 anchor_mechanism 块",
    "anchor_samples": "锚点图片应放在 anchor_mechanism 块",
    "anchor_mechanism": "锚点机制是独立块，不可嵌套在质量规则内",
    "dimensions": "八维定义应放在合同顶层 dimensions",
    "dimension_keys": "八维定义应放在合同顶层 dimensions",
    "dimension_weights": "维度权重应放在合同顶层 dimensions",
    "redline_policy": "红线策略应放在合同顶层 redline_policy",
    "redlines": "红线规则应放在合同顶层 redline_policy",
    "boundary_policy": "边界策略语义未定，且后端零实现，不得在此声明",
    "prompt_template": "提示词应放在提示词管理，不属于质量规则块",
    "call_b_version": "版本标识应放在合同顶层",
    "calibration_status": "标定状态应放在合同顶层",
    "aesthetic_foundation": "旧基座是被替代对象，不可嵌套",
}

_VALID_LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
# hard_defects 是调用 A 真实输出的画质硬伤列表，允许豁免（新增能力，旧配置不可能含此值）。
# content_defects 在调用 A 输出中不存在，装载时仍接受以免冻结配置在运行时失败；
# 新保存由 mechanism_authoring_policy 拦截。
_VALID_DEFECT_SOURCES = ("image_defects", "hard_defects", "content_defects")


class QualityRulesError(ValueError):
    """质量规则机制配置非法。"""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def assert_quality_rules_isolated(block: Any) -> None:
    """守卫单一职责：本块只准出现质量规则自己的字段。

    这不是注释约束，是机器可查的红线。混进外来机制会让调试失去可归因性。
    """
    if not isinstance(block, dict):
        raise QualityRulesError("quality_rules_not_object", "质量规则块必须是对象")

    for key, belongs_to in _FOREIGN_KEYS.items():
        if key in block:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "quality_rules_foreign_key",
                f"质量规则块不得包含 {key}（{belongs_to}）",
            )

    unknown = sorted(set(block) - _ALLOWED_BLOCK_FIELDS)
    if unknown:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "quality_rules_unknown_field",
            f"质量规则块出现未知字段：{', '.join(unknown)}",
        )

    snapshot = block.get("snapshot_limit")
    if snapshot is not None:
        if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
            raise QualityRulesError("snapshot_limit_not_object", "随手拍限分必须是对象")
        for key, belongs_to in _FOREIGN_KEYS.items():
            if key in snapshot:
                raise QualityRulesError(
                    "quality_rules_foreign_key",
                    f"随手拍限分不得包含 {key}（{belongs_to}）",
                )
        unknown = sorted(set(snapshot) - _ALLOWED_SNAPSHOT_FIELDS)
        if unknown:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "snapshot_limit_unknown_field",
                f"随手拍限分出现未知字段：{', '.join(unknown)}",
            )

    exceptions = block.get("defect_exceptions")
    if exceptions is not None:
        if not isinstance(exceptions, list):
            raise QualityRulesError("defect_exceptions_not_list", "硬伤例外名单必须是列表")
        for index, item in enumerate(exceptions):
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                raise QualityRulesError(
                    "defect_exception_not_object", f"硬伤例外第 {index + 1} 条必须是对象"
                )
            for key, belongs_to in _FOREIGN_KEYS.items():
                if key in item:
                    raise QualityRulesError(
                        "quality_rules_foreign_key",
                        f"硬伤例外第 {index + 1} 条不得包含 {key}（{belongs_to}）",
                    )
            unknown = sorted(set(item) - _ALLOWED_EXCEPTION_FIELDS)
            if unknown:
                raise QualityRulesError(
                    "defect_exception_unknown_field",
                    f"硬伤例外第 {index + 1} 条出现未知字段：{', '.join(unknown)}",
                )


def _validate_snapshot_limit(snapshot: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """校验并翻译随手拍限分为执行层内部形状。"""
    if snapshot.get("enabled") is False:
        return None

    reasons = snapshot.get("when_reason_contains")
    if not isinstance(reasons, list) or not reasons:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "snapshot_limit_reasons_empty", "随手拍限分至少要配一个判定理由关键词"
        )
    for item in reasons:
        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "snapshot_limit_reason_invalid", "判定理由关键词必须是非空文本"
            )

    signal = snapshot.get("signal") or "production_fields.reason"
    if not isinstance(signal, str) or not signal.strip():
        raise QualityRulesError("snapshot_limit_signal_invalid", "信号来源必须是非空文本")

    max_score = snapshot.get("max_score")
    max_level = snapshot.get("max_level")
    if max_score is None and max_level is None:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "snapshot_limit_target_missing", "随手拍限分要么配最高分数，要么配最高等级"
        )
    if max_score is not None and max_level is not None:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "snapshot_limit_target_conflict", "最高分数与最高等级只能配一个"
        )

    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {
        "key": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        "signal": signal,
        "match_any": [str(item) for item in reasons],
    }

    if max_score is not None:
        if not isinstance(max_score, int) or isinstance(max_score, bool):
            raise QualityRulesError("snapshot_limit_score_invalid", "最高分数必须是整数")
        if not 0 <= max_score <= 100:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "snapshot_limit_score_range", "最高分数必须在 0 到 100 之间"
            )
        normalized["cap_to"] = max_score
        return normalized

    if max_level not in _VALID_LEVELS:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "snapshot_limit_level_invalid",
            f"最高等级必须是 {'/'.join(_VALID_LEVELS)} 之一",
        )
    normalized["cap_to_level"] = max_level

    ceilings = snapshot.get("dimension_ceilings")
    if ceilings is None:
        ceilings = {}
    if not isinstance(ceilings, dict):
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "snapshot_limit_ceilings_invalid", "维度分上限必须是对象"
        )
    normalized_ceilings: dict[str, int] = {}
    for dimension_key, limit in ceilings.items():
        if not isinstance(dimension_key, str) or not dimension_key.strip():
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "snapshot_limit_ceiling_key_invalid", "维度分上限的维度名必须是非空文本"
            )
        if not isinstance(limit, int) or isinstance(limit, bool) or not 1 <= limit <= 5:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "snapshot_limit_ceiling_value_invalid",
                f"维度 {dimension_key} 的分上限必须是 1 到 5 的整数",
            )
        normalized_ceilings[dimension_key] = limit

    normalized["filter_escalation"] = {
        "cap_to_level": max_level,
        "dimensions_at_most": normalized_ceilings,
    }
    return normalized


def _validate_defect_exception(item: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """校验并翻译单条硬伤例外为执行层内部形状。"""
    position = f"硬伤例外第 {index + 1} 条"

    name = item.get("name")
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "defect_exception_name_invalid", f"{position}要有名字，便于运营辨认"
        )

    defect = item.get("defect")
    if not isinstance(defect, str) or not defect.strip():
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "defect_exception_defect_invalid", f"{position}要指明豁免哪个硬伤"
        )

    source = item.get("defect_source") or "image_defects"
    if source not in _VALID_DEFECT_SOURCES:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "defect_exception_source_invalid",
            f"{position}的硬伤来源必须是 {'/'.join(_VALID_DEFECT_SOURCES)} 之一",
        )

    evidence = item.get("when_evidence_contains")
    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "defect_exception_evidence_empty", f"{position}至少要配一个佐证关键词"
        )
    for token in evidence:
        if not isinstance(token, str) or not token.strip():
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_evidence_invalid", f"{position}的佐证关键词必须是非空文本"
            )

    requirements = item.get("require_dimensions")
    if not isinstance(requirements, list) or not requirements:
        raise QualityRulesError(
            "defect_exception_requirements_empty",
            f"{position}至少要配一条维度门槛，避免无条件豁免",
        )

    normalized_requirements: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for requirement in requirements:
        if not isinstance(requirement, dict):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_invalid", f"{position}的维度门槛必须是对象"
            )
        unknown = sorted(set(requirement) - _ALLOWED_REQUIREMENT_FIELDS)
        if unknown:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_unknown_field",
                f"{position}的维度门槛出现未知字段：{', '.join(unknown)}",
            )
        dimension_key = requirement.get("dimension")
        if not isinstance(dimension_key, str) or not dimension_key.strip():
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_dimension_invalid",
                f"{position}的维度门槛要指明维度",
            )
        if dimension_key in normalized_requirements:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_duplicate",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 重复配置",
            )
        # 两类门槛，各绑定一条评测路径的证据：
        # - min_grade / no_shortcomings：调用B输出八维档位的路径（1-5 档 + 缺点清单）
        # - no_deduction_hits / max_deduction：规则计分路径（每维累计扣分现成可得）
        # 每条 requirement 至少要配一类，否则等于无条件豁免。
        min_grade = requirement.get("min_grade")
        if min_grade is not None and (
            not isinstance(min_grade, int)
            or isinstance(min_grade, bool)
            or not 1 <= min_grade <= 5
        ):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_grade_invalid",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 最低档位必须是 1 到 5 的整数",
            )
        no_shortcomings = requirement.get("no_shortcomings", False)
        if not isinstance(no_shortcomings, bool):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_flag_invalid",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 的「不能有缺点」必须是真假值",
            )
        no_deduction_hits = requirement.get("no_deduction_hits", False)
        if not isinstance(no_deduction_hits, bool):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_flag_invalid",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 的「未命中扣分规则」必须是真假值",
            )
        max_deduction = requirement.get("max_deduction")
        if max_deduction is not None and (
            isinstance(max_deduction, bool)
            or not isinstance(max_deduction, (int, float))
            or not 0 <= max_deduction <= 100
        ):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_deduction_invalid",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 最大扣分必须是 0 到 100 的数值",
            )
        if (
            min_grade is None
            and not no_shortcomings
            and not no_deduction_hits
            and max_deduction is None
        ):
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_requirement_empty",
                f"{position}的维度 {dimension_key} 至少要配一种门槛"
                "（最低档位 / 不能有缺点 / 未命中扣分规则 / 最大扣分）",
            )
        normalized: dict[str, Any] = {
            "shortcomings_empty": no_shortcomings,
        }
        if min_grade is not None:
            normalized["min_grade"] = min_grade
        if no_deduction_hits:
            normalized["no_deduction_hits"] = True
        if max_deduction is not None:
            normalized["max_deduction"] = float(max_deduction)
        normalized_requirements[dimension_key] = normalized

    return {
        "key": name.strip(),
        "source": source,
        "defect_key": defect.strip(),
        "evidence_contains_any": [str(token) for token in evidence],
        "foundation_requirements": normalized_requirements,
    }


def load_quality_rules(
    contract: dict[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, list[dict[str, Any]]] | None:
    """从合同装载质量规则，翻译成执行层内部形状。

    返回 ``None`` 表示合同没有本块（调用方应回落旧路径）；
    返回 ``(soft_cap, exemptions)``，与执行层既有消费形状一致。
    """
    if not isinstance(contract, dict):
        raise QualityRulesError("contract_not_object", "合同必须是对象")

    block = contract.get(CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY)
    if block is None:
        return None

    assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)

    if block.get("enabled") is False:
        return None, []

    snapshot = block.get("snapshot_limit")
    normalized_snapshot: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    if isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        normalized_snapshot = _validate_snapshot_limit(snapshot)

    exceptions = block.get("defect_exceptions") or []
    normalized_exceptions = [
        _validate_defect_exception(item, index) for index, item in enumerate(exceptions)
    ]

    seen_keys: set[str] = set()
    for item in normalized_exceptions:
        if item["key"] in seen_keys:
            raise QualityRulesError(
                "defect_exception_name_duplicate", f"硬伤例外名字重复：{item['key']}"
            )
        seen_keys.add(item["key"])

    return normalized_snapshot, normalized_exceptions


def validate_quality_rules_block(contract: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """合同保存前校验：只做校验，不返回装载结果。"""
    load_quality_rules(contract)


def default_quality_rules_block() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """给前端与种子数据用的默认块，语义等价于当前生产配置。"""
    return copy.deepcopy({
        "enabled": True,
        "snapshot_limit": {
            "enabled": True,
            "name": "随手拍限分",
            "signal": "production_fields.reason",
            "when_reason_contains": ["是随手拍"],
            "max_score": 59,
        },
        "defect_exceptions": [
            {
                "name": "subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark",
                "defect": "subject_obscuring_watermark",
                "defect_source": "image_defects",
                "when_evidence_contains": ["品牌文字", "品牌字样"],
                "require_dimensions": [
                    {
                        "dimension": "detail_completion",
                        "min_grade": 4,
                        "no_shortcomings": True,
                    },
                    {
                        "dimension": "presentation_integrity",
                        "min_grade": 4,
                        "no_shortcomings": True,
                    },
                ],
            }
        ],
    })

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_rule_priority.py =====
"""Repair the F2 candidate's overlapping terminal rules without changing facts."""
from copy import deepcopy


def prioritize_inspiration_quality_guard(manifest: dict) -> dict:
    result = deepcopy(manifest)
    assert result['category_key'] == 'inspiration_image'
    node = next(n for n in result['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_a')
    rules = node['config']['short_circuit_rules']
    by_key = {r['rule_key']: r for r in rules}
    guard = by_key['inspiration-hard-defect-l5-v3']
    track = by_key['inspiration-class-three-l4-v2']
    assert (guard['target_level'], guard['priority']) == ('L5', 900)
    assert (track['target_level'], track['priority']) == ('L4', 900)
    assert all(r['priority'] == 1000 and r['target_level'] == 'L5'
               for r in rules if r['rule_key'].startswith('inspiration-redline-'))
    guard['priority'] = 950
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_semantic_consistency.py =====
"""Cross-field semantic checks for strict inspiration prompt outputs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import json
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .schema_adapter import adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck
from .strict_prompt_contracts import (
    LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    LATEST_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_SIGNALS,
    merged_strict_output_contract,
    strict_output_contract,
)


_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_NAMES = frozenset(
    {"inspiration_call_a", "inspiration_latest_single"}
)
LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS = (
    "visual_structure",
    "color_aesthetics",
    "emotional_expression",
    "design_aesthetics",
    "originality",
    "design_trend_popularity",
)
_CROSS_FIELD_CONFLICT_PREFIXES = (
    "redline_reason_conflict:",
    "missing_evidence:redline:",
    "evidence_conflict:redline:",
    "missing_evidence:hard_defects:",
    "evidence_without_hit:hard_defects:",
)


def _latest_redline_policy() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "format_version": "redline-policy-v1",
        "enabled": True,
        "hit_level": "L5",
        "rules": [
            {
                "key": key,
                "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                "match_any": list(reasons),
                "exemptions": [],
                "enabled": True,
            }
            for key, reasons in LATEST_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_SIGNALS
        ],
    }


def frozen_inspiration_contract_profile(
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> str | None:
    """Read the immutable inspiration profile from the run's workflow snapshot."""

    workflow = run_snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    resource_policy = (
        manifest.get("resource_policy") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    )
    provenance = (
        resource_policy.get("source_provenance")
        if isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    profile = (
        provenance.get("contract_profile")
        if isinstance(provenance, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    return profile if isinstance(profile, str) and profile else None


def _latest_semantic_schema_matches(
    contract_name: str,
    output_schema: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    """Bind the profile to the exact frozen A or merged A+B strict schema."""

    evaluation_contract = {
        "image_defects_enabled": False,
        "aesthetic_foundation": {
            "dimension_keys": list(
                LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS
            ),
        },
    }
    if contract_name == "inspiration_call_a":
        expected = strict_output_contract(
            "inspiration_image",
            "A",
            evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
            contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        )["schema"]
    elif contract_name == "inspiration_latest_single":
        expected = merged_strict_output_contract(
            "inspiration_image",
            ["A", "B"],
            evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
            name="inspiration_latest_single",
            description="semantic identity only",
            contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        )["schema"]
    else:
        return False
    return dict(output_schema) == expected


def inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
    contract_name: str,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    contract_profile: str | None,
    output_schema: Mapping[str, Any],
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    node: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Return retryable cross-field conflicts without changing model output."""

    # Explicit opt-in on a new immutable mechanism. Existing runs retain their
    # exact-schema behavior; custom exemption schemas use their own frozen rules.
    manifest = ((run_snapshot or {}).get("workflow") or {}).get("manifest") or {}
    # Only a new, exact bounded C contract opts into metadata repair. Its usage
    # is settled by the existing executor before a bounded retry is scheduled.
    if (contract_name == "inspiration_production_metadata"
            and manifest.get("category_key") == "inspiration_image"
            and ((node or {}).get("config") or {}).get("execution_role") == "production_metadata"):
        from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract, metadata_text_conflicts, postcheck_enabled
        from .inspiration_metadata_generation import metadata_contract
        if (dict(output_schema) == bounded_contract(metadata_contract())["schema"]
                or postcheck_enabled(manifest) and dict(output_schema) == metadata_contract()["schema"]):
            return metadata_text_conflicts(payload)
    provenance = (manifest.get("resource_policy") or {}).get("source_provenance") or {}
    custom = provenance.get("semantic_validation_profile") == "inspiration-frozen-redline-v1"
    if custom:
        if manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image":
            raise ValueError("Frozen inspiration semantic profile on another category")
        if ((node or {}).get("config") or {}).get("asset_key") != "prompt_a":
            return ()
        if contract_name != "inspiration_call_a" or dict(output_schema) != (node or {}).get("output_schema"):
            raise ValueError("Frozen A schema identity mismatch")
        policies = [n["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"]
                    for n in manifest.get("nodes", [])
                    if n.get("kind") == "rule_module"
                    and (n.get("config") or {}).get("module_type_key") == "eligibility_redline"]
        if len(policies) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Exactly one frozen redline policy required")
        policy = json.loads(policies[0])
        if (policy.get("format_version") != "redline-policy-v1"
                or policy.get("authoritative_precheck_contract", {}).get("required_validation_status") != "valid"):
            raise ValueError("Frozen authoritative redline policy required")
        adapted = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(deepcopy(dict(payload)),
            redline_policy=policy, image_defects_enabled=False)
        validation = adapted.get("decisive_signal_validation") or {}
        return tuple(dict.fromkeys(reason for reason in validation.get("reasons", [])
            if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.startswith(_CROSS_FIELD_CONFLICT_PREFIXES)))

    if (
        contract_name not in _INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_NAMES
        or contract_profile != LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
        or not _latest_semantic_schema_matches(contract_name, output_schema)
    ):
        return ()
    adapted = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
        deepcopy(dict(payload)),
        redline_policy=_latest_redline_policy(),
        image_defects_enabled=False,
    )
    validation = adapted.get("decisive_signal_validation")
    reasons = validation.get("reasons") if isinstance(validation, dict) else []
    conflicts: list[str] = []
    for reason in reasons if isinstance(reasons, list) else []:
        if (
            isinstance(reason, str)
            and reason.startswith(_CROSS_FIELD_CONFLICT_PREFIXES)
            and reason not in conflicts
        ):
            conflicts.append(reason)
    return tuple(conflicts)

from typing import Literal


InvariantOwner = Literal["database_trigger", "orm_guard"]
InvariantEvent = Literal["UPDATE", "DELETE"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvariantContract:
    name: str
    owner: InvariantOwner
    rationale: str
    table_name: str | None = None
    event: InvariantEvent | None = None
    message: str | None = None

def _invariant_contract(name: str) -> InvariantContract:
    details = _DATABASE_TRIGGER_DETAILS.get(name)
    if details is None:
        return InvariantContract(
            name=name,
            owner="orm_guard",
            rationale=(
                "由现有模型事件、服务状态机和表级约束"
                "共同保护复杂业务转换"
            ),
        )
    table_name, event, message = details
    return InvariantContract(
        name=name,
        owner="database_trigger",
        rationale=(
            "数据库触发器直接保护不可变、不可更新或不可删除的事实"
        ),
        table_name=table_name,
        event=event,
        message=message,
    )


INVARIANT_CATALOG = {
    name: _invariant_contract(name)
    for name in _FINAL_SQLITE_TRIGGER_NAMES
}


if len(INVARIANT_CATALOG) != len(_FINAL_SQLITE_TRIGGER_NAMES):
    raise RuntimeError("不变量目录包含重复名称")
if not DATABASE_TRIGGER_INVARIANT_NAMES <= set(INVARIANT_CATALOG):
    raise RuntimeError("数据库触发器归属包含未登记不变量")
if set(_DATABASE_TRIGGER_DETAILS) != DATABASE_TRIGGER_INVARIANT_NAMES:
    raise RuntimeError("数据库触发器语义明细与归属目录不一致")

===== FILE backend/app/invocation_review.py =====
"""Explicit historical provider-execution closure, independent of billing facts."""
import hashlib
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import load_only
from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .models import ModelInvocationReview, WorkflowModelInvocation, ProductionRun
from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError

UNKNOWN = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
TERMINAL = ("succeeded", "failed", "canceled")


def fingerprint(call):
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
        "id":call.id,"run":call.production_run_id,"status":call.status,
        "error_code":call.error_code,"request_id":call.request_correlation_id,
        "input_tokens":call.input_tokens,"output_tokens":call.output_tokens,
        "estimated_cost_micros":call.estimated_cost_micros,"actual_cost_micros":call.actual_cost_micros,
        "evidence":call.evidence_json}).encode()).hexdigest()


def list_unknown_invocations(db, *, category_key=None, reviewed=False, limit=25, offset=0):
    where=[WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code==UNKNOWN,
        ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id.is_not(None) if reviewed else ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id.is_(None)]
    if category_key:where.append(ProductionRun.category_key==category_key)
    base=select(WorkflowModelInvocation,ProductionRun,ModelInvocationReview).join(
        ProductionRun,ProductionRun.id==WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id).outerjoin(
        ModelInvocationReview,ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id==WorkflowModelInvocation.id).where(*where)
    total=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(base.with_only_columns(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).subquery())) or 0
    items=[]
    rows = base.options(load_only(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.status))
    for call,run,review in db.execute(rows.order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(limit).offset(offset)):
        items.append({"id":call.id,"production_run_id":run.id,"category_key":run.category_key,
            "step":call.node_instance_id,"run_status":run.status,"provider":call.provider,
            "request_id":call.request_correlation_id,"created_at":call.created_at,
            "quote_hash":fingerprint(call),"estimated_cost_micros":call.estimated_cost_micros,
            "can_review":review is None and call.status=="failed" and run.status in TERMINAL,
            "billing_status":"pending_reconciliation",
            "review":None if review is None else {"actor":review.actor,"reason":review.reason,
                "evidence_reference":review.evidence_reference,"created_at":review.created_at}})
    return {"items":items,"total":total,"limit":limit,"offset":offset,"has_more":offset+len(items)<total}


def review_unknown_invocation(db, *, invocation_id, quote_hash, evidence_reference, reason,
                              execution_ended_confirmed, liability_retained_confirmed, actor):
    if (execution_ended_confirmed is not True or liability_retained_confirmed is not True
            or not evidence_reference.strip() or not reason.strip()):
        raise FailureConsoleError("invocation_review_evidence_required","须填写核验依据，并确认请求已结束及原费用责任保留",status_code=422)
    call=db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id==invocation_id)
        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if call is None:raise FailureConsoleError("invocation_not_found","调用不存在",status_code=404)
    prior=db.get(ModelInvocationReview,call.id)
    if prior:
        if (prior.invocation_fingerprint!=quote_hash or prior.reason!=reason.strip()
                or prior.evidence_reference!=evidence_reference.strip()):
            raise FailureConsoleError("invocation_review_conflict","已存在不同的核验记录",status_code=409)
        return {"invocation_id":call.id,"reviewed":True,"idempotent_replay":True,"billing_status":"pending_reconciliation"}
    run=db.get(ProductionRun,call.production_run_id)
    if call.status!="failed" or call.error_code!=UNKNOWN or run is None or run.status not in TERMINAL:
        raise FailureConsoleError("invocation_review_active","仅允许核验已终止运行的未知调用；在途请求必须自然完成",status_code=409)
    if fingerprint(call)!=quote_hash:
        raise FailureConsoleError("invocation_review_stale","调用记录已变化，请刷新后核验",status_code=409)
    row=ModelInvocationReview(invocation_id=call.id,invocation_fingerprint=quote_hash,
        evidence_reference=evidence_reference.strip(),reason=reason.strip(),actor=actor)
    db.add(row)
    append_audit_event(db,category="model_invocation",action="historical_execution_reviewed",
        subject_type="workflow_model_invocation",subject_id=call.id,actor=actor,
        event_key=f"invocation-review:{call.id}",payload={"fingerprint":quote_hash,
            "execution_ended_confirmed":True,"liability_retained_confirmed":True,
            "evidence_reference":row.evidence_reference,"reason":row.reason,
            "billing_status":"pending_reconciliation","model_output_changed":False,"automatic_retry":False})
    db.flush()
    return {"invocation_id":call.id,"reviewed":True,"idempotent_replay":False,"billing_status":"pending_reconciliation"}

===== FILE backend/app/joint_model_workflow.py =====
"""Explicit candidate creation for shared 3DSU classification and KG labeling.

Never called at startup; never enables incremental processing or downstreams.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .audit import canonical_json
from .knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    knowledge_graph_contract_catalog, validate_knowledge_graph_output,
)
from .strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract
from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, PromptVersion, RuleModuleVersion, WorkflowNodeAsset, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion

CATEGORY = 'model_3d_su_joint'
_ROUTE_MARKER = '\n【冻结联合知识图谱路线v1】\n'


def freeze_joint_prompt(base, routes):
    if _ROUTE_MARKER in base:
        raise ValueError('joint_prompt_already_combined')
    return base + _ROUTE_MARKER + canonical_json(routes)


def render_joint_prompt(template, input_identity):
    key = validate_joint_input(input_identity)
    contract = knowledge_graph_contract_catalog('knowledge_graph')[key]
    if template.count(_ROUTE_MARKER) != 1:
        raise ValueError('joint_prompt_route_snapshot_invalid')
    base, encoded = template.split(_ROUTE_MARKER)
    routes = json.loads(encoded)
    selected = routes[contract.site_scope+'_'+contract.asset_scope]
    return (base + '\n本次联合知识图谱职责（只选择当前输入路线）：\n' + selected +
            '\n最终输出以联合JSON Schema为准：原有A字段继续完整返回，知识图谱结果放在knowledge_graph.fields中，'
            'knowledge_graph.contract_key填写'+key+'。不属于当前路线的ll_id/res_id/title填写空字符串。'
            '美感短路也必须输出完整知识图谱。只能输出一个联合JSON对象。')


def joint_output_contract(base=None):
    contract = deepcopy(base or strict_output_contract('model_3d_su', 'A', evaluation_contract={}))
    fields = {}
    keys = []
    for key, kg in knowledge_graph_contract_catalog('knowledge_graph').items():
        keys.append(key)
        fields.update(deepcopy(kg.output_schema['properties']))
    fields['is_single'] = {'type': 'integer', 'enum': [0, 1, 2]}
    for name in ('ll_id', 'res_id', 'title'):
        fields[name] = {'type': 'string'}
    contract['name'] = 'model_3d_su_joint_call_a'
    contract['schema']['properties']['knowledge_graph'] = {
        'type': 'object', 'additionalProperties': False, 'required': ['contract_key', 'fields'],
        'properties': {'contract_key': {'type': 'string', 'enum': keys},
                       'fields': {'type': 'object', 'additionalProperties': False,
                                  'properties': fields, 'required': list(fields)}}}
    contract['schema']['required'].append('knowledge_graph')
    return contract


def validate_joint_input(value):
    source = value.get('source')
    scope = value.get('is_single')
    if source not in {'domestic','overseas'} or type(scope) is not int:
        raise ValueError('joint_input_route_invalid')
    matches = [c for c in knowledge_graph_contract_catalog('knowledge_graph').values()
               if c.site_scope == source and c.is_single == scope]
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise ValueError('joint_input_route_invalid')
    contract = matches[0]
    identity = value.get(contract.identity_field)
    other = 'res_id' if source == 'domestic' else 'll_id'
    if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip() or other in value:
        raise ValueError('joint_input_identity_invalid')
    return contract.contract_key


def validate_joint_knowledge(payload, *, expected_input=None):
    kg = payload.get('knowledge_graph')
    if not isinstance(kg, dict) or set(kg) != {'contract_key', 'fields'}:
        raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_missing')
    contracts = knowledge_graph_contract_catalog('knowledge_graph')
    key = kg['contract_key']
    if not isinstance(key, str) or key not in contracts:
        raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_route_invalid')
    c = contracts[key]
    fields = kg['fields']
    if not isinstance(fields, dict):
        raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_fields_invalid')
    identity = fields.get(c.identity_field)
    if expected_input is not None:
        validate_joint_input(expected_input)
        if expected_input.get('source') != c.site_scope or expected_input.get('is_single') != c.is_single:
            raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_input_route_mismatch')
        identity = expected_input.get(c.identity_field)
        if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip():
            raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_input_identity_missing')
    if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip():
        raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_identity_missing')
    extra = set(fields) - set(c.field_order)
    if any(name not in {'ll_id', 'res_id', 'title'} or fields[name] != '' for name in extra):
        raise ValueError('joint_knowledge_graph_unrelated_fields')
    selected = {name: fields[name] for name in c.field_order if name in fields}
    validate_knowledge_graph_output(key, selected, expected_asset_id=identity)
    return {'contract_key': key, 'fields': deepcopy(selected)}


def _copy_row(row, **overrides):
    # Copy declarative content only; IDs/timestamps and caller-selected lineage differ.
    values = {c.name: getattr(row, c.name) for c in row.__table__.columns
              if c.name not in {'id', 'created_at', 'updated_at'}}
    values.update(overrides)
    return type(row)(**values)


def create_joint_candidate(db: Session, *, source: WorkflowVersion, actor: str):
    from .workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition, create_workflow_version, persisted_workflow_manifest
    from .workflow_assets import bind_prompt_version, bind_rule_module_version
    from .workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references
    manifest = deepcopy(persisted_workflow_manifest(source))
    if manifest.get('category_key') != 'model_3d_su' or manifest.get('schema_version') != 'workflow-v3':
        raise ValueError('joint_source_requires_native_3dsu')
    prompts = [n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'prompt']
    a = next((n for n in prompts if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_a'), None)
    if a is None or not a['config'].get('output_contract', {}).get('schema'):
        raise ValueError('joint_source_requires_strict_a')
    # Preserve A even for upstream short circuits: unsupported source shapes fail closed.
    for n in manifest['nodes']:
        if n['node_instance_id'] == 'upstream' and ('short_circuit' in canonical_json(n['config'])):
            raise ValueError('joint_upstream_short_circuit_requires_explicit_adaptation')
    version = 'joint-v1-' + source.canonical_hash[:16]
    definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == CATEGORY+'.evaluation'))
    if definition:
        existing = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id==definition.id, WorkflowVersion.version==version))
        if existing:
            return existing
    profile = db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key==CATEGORY))
    if profile is None:
        original = db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key=='model_3d_su'))
        if original is None:
            raise ValueError('joint_source_category_missing')
        profile = _copy_row(original, category_key=CATEGORY, display_name='3D/SU联合打标',
                            description='共享分类A，分别产出美感定级及完整知识图谱字段。候选需回归验收。',
                            prompt_a_id=None, prompt_b_id=None, automation_last_triggered_at=None,
                            automation_config_json=canonical_json({**json.loads(original.automation_config_json or '{}'), 'enabled':False}),
                            created_by=actor)
        db.add(profile); db.flush()
    if definition is None:
        definition = create_workflow_definition(db, workflow_key=CATEGORY+'.evaluation', name='3D/SU美感与知识图谱联合工作流',
            description='共享A，短路保留知识图谱；独立候选，不自动启用增量。', owner='platform', allowed_categories=[CATEGORY], created_by=actor)
    for n in manifest['nodes']:
        cfg = n['config']
        if n['kind'] in {'upstream_connector', 'downstream_projection'}:
            cfg['connector_key'] = 'logical.'+CATEGORY+('.input' if n['kind']=='upstream_connector' else '.output')
            continue
        if n['kind'] not in {'prompt', 'rule_module'}:
            continue
        old_asset = db.scalar(select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key=='model_3d_su', WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key==cfg['asset_key']))
        if old_asset is None:
            raise ValueError('joint_source_asset_missing')
        asset_key = cfg['asset_key']+'_'+source.canonical_hash[:8]
        asset = _copy_row(old_asset, category_key=CATEGORY, asset_key=asset_key, created_by=actor)
        db.add(asset); db.flush()
        cfg['asset_key'] = asset_key
        if n['kind'] == 'prompt':
            if n['node_instance_id'] in {'prompt_a', 'prompt_b'}:
                cfg.setdefault('execution_role', 'classification' if n is a else 'grading')
            old = db.get(PromptVersion, cfg['prompt_version_id'])
            row = _copy_row(old, category_key=CATEGORY, version=version+'-'+str(old.id), status='draft',
                rollback_prompt_id=old.id, source_optimization_run_id=None, source_automation_run_id=None, created_by=actor)
            if n is a:
                cfg['output_contract'] = joint_output_contract(cfg['output_contract'])
                from .native_workflow_migration import knowledge_graph_prompt_sources
                row.system_prompt = freeze_joint_prompt(row.system_prompt, knowledge_graph_prompt_sources())
            db.add(row); db.flush()
            cfg['prompt_version_id'] = row.id
            if n is a and profile.prompt_a_id is None:
                profile.prompt_a_id = row.id
            elif n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_b' and profile.prompt_b_id is None:
                profile.prompt_b_id = row.id
            bind_prompt_version(db, asset=asset, prompt_version=row, created_by=actor)
        else:
            old = db.get(RuleModuleVersion, cfg['rule_module_version_id'])
            row = _copy_row(old, asset_id=asset.id, created_by=actor)
            db.add(row); db.flush()
            cfg['rule_module_version_id'] = row.id
            bind_rule_module_version(db, asset=asset, rule_module_version=row, created_by=actor)
    manifest['category_key'] = CATEGORY
    for n in manifest['nodes']:
        if n['kind'] == 'downstream_projection':
            n['config']['mapping'] = {'schema_version':'joint-model-projection-v1'}
    projection = deepcopy(manifest['resource_policy'].get('legacy_projection_snapshot'))
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    if projection:
        frozen['resource_policy']['legacy_projection_snapshot'] = projection
    frozen['resource_policy']['joint_source'] = {'workflow_version_id':source.id, 'canonical_hash':source.canonical_hash}
    result = create_workflow_version(db, definition=definition, version=version, manifest=frozen, created_by=actor)
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/knowledge_graph_category_seed.py =====
"""Idempotent seed for the independent knowledge-graph labeling category."""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .category_pipeline import default_pipeline
from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, ModelConfig


KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CATEGORY_KEY = "knowledge_graph"


def ensure_knowledge_graph_category(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> EvaluationCategoryProfile:
    """Return the one public KG category without mutating legacy 3D&SU rows."""

    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key
            == KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CATEGORY_KEY
        )
    )
    if profile is not None:
        return profile
    model = db.scalar(
        select(ModelConfig)
        .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
        .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
    )
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key=KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CATEGORY_KEY,
        display_name="知识图谱打标",
        description=(
            "按国内/海外、整体/单体四条路线生成结构化字段；"
            "存量回归使用导入素材，生产连接器单独绑定。"
        ),
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json=(
            '["image/jpeg","image/png","image/webp","image/gif"]'
        ),
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        pipeline_config_json=canonical_json(
            default_pipeline(KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CATEGORY_KEY)
        ),
        model_config_id=model.id if model is not None else None,
        rubric_version="knowledge-graph-v1",
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(profile)
    db.flush()
    return profile

===== FILE backend/app/knowledge_graph_contracts.py =====
"""Knowledge-graph base contracts and immutable candidate field extensions.

Catalog keys identify routes; frozen contract hashes identify exact schemas.
Machine clients may extend candidate fields while retaining base semantics.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json


KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS = (
    "space",
    "object",
    "style",
    "material",
    "structural_features",
    "architectural_element",
    "soft_decoration",
    "hard_decoration",
    "color",
    "form",
    "structure",
)

_OBJECT_WEIGHT_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^(?P<value>.+)_(?P<weight>0\.[0-9]+)$")
_WHOLE_OBJECT_WEIGHTS = (0.7, 0.5, 0.3)
_MULTI_VALUE_LIMITS = {
    "object": 3,
    "style": 2,
    "material": 3,
    "structural_features": 3,
    "architectural_element": 3,
    "soft_decoration": 3,
    "hard_decoration": 3,
    "form": 3,
    "structure": 3,
}

_FIELD_LABELS = {
    "view_angle": ("视角", "相机观察主体的角度；无法判断时返回空字符串"),
    "ll_id": ("国内素材 ID", "原样返回国内链路传入的 ll_id"),
    "is_single": ("整体/单体", "国内 0=整体 1=单体；海外 1=整体 2=单体"),
    "res_id": ("海外素材 ID", "原样返回海外链路传入的 res_id"),
    "title": ("英文标题", "海外链路根据可见内容生成的简短英文标题"),
    "space": ("空间类型", "画面中可靠识别的空间类型"),
    "object": ("主要物体", "画面中主要且可直接识别的物体"),
    "style": ("风格", "画面可靠呈现的整体或单体风格"),
    "material": ("主要材质", "从表面外观可靠判断的主要材质"),
    "structural_features": ("结构特征", "主体明确可见的构造或产品结构特征"),
    "architectural_element": ("建筑构件", "画面中明确可见的固定建筑或室内构件"),
    "soft_decoration": ("软装元素", "画面中明确可见的可移动装饰、织物或陈设"),
    "hard_decoration": ("硬装元素", "画面中明确可见的固定装修面层或造型"),
    "color": ("主色系", "画面中占比最高且可靠判断的主色系"),
    "form": ("视觉形态", "从受控词表选择的主导视觉形态"),
    "structure": ("组织关系", "从受控词表选择的主导组织或构成关系"),
}


def field_label(field: str) -> str:
    """字段的中文名，导出和页面共用同一套说法。未登记字段回落到原名。"""

    entry = _FIELD_LABELS.get(field)
    return entry[0] if entry else field


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class KnowledgeGraphOutputContract:
    contract_key: str
    site_scope: str
    asset_scope: str
    locale: str
    identity_field: str
    is_single: int
    field_order: tuple[str, ...]
    _output_schema_json: str

    @property
    def output_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return json.loads(self._output_schema_json)

    def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload = {
            "schema_version": "kg-model-output-contract-v1",
            "contract_key": self.contract_key,
            "route": {
                "site_scope": self.site_scope,
                "asset_scope": self.asset_scope,
                "locale": self.locale,
            },
            "identity_field": self.identity_field,
            "is_single": self.is_single,
            "field_order": list(self.field_order),
            "output_schema": self.output_schema,
        }
        payload["contract_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        return payload


class KnowledgeGraphContractError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _schema(
    *,
    field_order: tuple[str, ...],
    is_single: int,
    site_scope: str,
    asset_scope: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    properties: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for field in field_order:
        if field == "is_single":
            route = "国内" if site_scope == "domestic" else "海外"
            scope = "整体" if asset_scope == "whole" else "单体"
            properties[field] = {
                "type": "integer",
                "enum": [is_single],
                "title": "是否单体",
                "description": f"{route}{scope}链路固定为 {is_single}",
            }
            continue
        title, description = _FIELD_LABELS[field]
        properties[field] = {
            "type": "string",
            "title": title,
            "description": description,
        }
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": properties,
        "required": list(field_order),
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


# is_single 是 asset_scope 的站点相关投影别名（ADR-0047：asset_scope 才是 Canonical）。
# 2026-09-02 老大定：海外整体=1、单体=2，国内保持整体=0、单体=1。
# 海外历史数据（2026-09-02 前）用的是 0/1，读取时按合同键自愈到新编码。
IS_SINGLE_CODES: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "domestic": MappingProxyType({"whole": 0, "single": 1}),
        "overseas": MappingProxyType({"whole": 1, "single": 2}),
    }
)
_LEGACY_IS_SINGLE_CODES: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]] = MappingProxyType(
    {"overseas": MappingProxyType({"whole": 0, "single": 1})}
)


def is_single_code(site_scope: str, asset_scope: str) -> int:
    try:
        return IS_SINGLE_CODES[site_scope][asset_scope]
    except KeyError as exc:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_route_invalid",
            f"未知的知识图谱路线：{site_scope}/{asset_scope}",
        ) from exc


def is_single_codes(site_scope: str) -> frozenset[int]:
    """该站点当前允许的 is_single 取值（不含历史别名）。"""

    return frozenset(IS_SINGLE_CODES.get(site_scope, {}).values())


def asset_scope_from_is_single(site_scope: str, value: Any) -> str | None:
    """按站点把 is_single 反解成 whole/single；非法或布尔值返回 None。"""

    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
        return None
    for scope, code in IS_SINGLE_CODES.get(site_scope, {}).items():
        if code == value:
            return scope
    return None


def normalize_is_single_for_contract(
    contract: "KnowledgeGraphOutputContract", value: Any
) -> int | None:
    """合同键已明确路线时，把当前编码或该路线的历史编码归一到当前编码。

    返回 None 表示取值与该路线无关（既不是当前编码也不是历史别名）。
    """

    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
        return None
    if value == contract.is_single:
        return contract.is_single
    legacy = _LEGACY_IS_SINGLE_CODES.get(contract.site_scope, {}).get(
        contract.asset_scope
    )
    if legacy is not None and value == legacy:
        return contract.is_single
    return None


def _contract(
    category_key: str,
    site_scope: str,
    asset_scope: str,
    version: str = "v1",
) -> KnowledgeGraphOutputContract:
    domestic = site_scope == "domestic"
    identity_field = "ll_id" if domestic else "res_id"
    is_single = is_single_code(site_scope, asset_scope)
    field_order = (
        identity_field,
        "is_single",
        *(("title",) if not domestic else ()),
        *KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS,
        *(("view_angle",) if version == "v2" else ()),
    )
    contract_key = f"kg.{category_key}.{site_scope}.{asset_scope}.{version}"
    output_schema = _schema(
        field_order=field_order,
        is_single=is_single,
        site_scope=site_scope,
        asset_scope=asset_scope,
    )
    if version == "v2":
        output_schema["properties"]["view_angle"]["enum"] = (
            ["", "正视", "侧视", "斜视", "俯视斜角", "鸟瞰", "顶视", "仰视"]
            if domestic else
            ["", "front view", "side view", "oblique view", "elevated oblique view",
             "bird's-eye view", "top view", "low-angle view"]
        )
    return KnowledgeGraphOutputContract(
        contract_key=contract_key,
        site_scope=site_scope,
        asset_scope=asset_scope,
        locale="zh" if domestic else "en",
        identity_field=identity_field,
        is_single=is_single,
        field_order=field_order,
        _output_schema_json=canonical_json(output_schema),
    )


_SUPPORTED_CATEGORIES = ("model_3d_su", "knowledge_graph")
_CATALOG_BY_CATEGORY = {
    category_key: {
        contract.contract_key: contract
        for contract in (
            _contract(category_key, "domestic", "whole"),
            _contract(category_key, "domestic", "single"),
            _contract(category_key, "overseas", "whole"),
            _contract(category_key, "overseas", "single"),
        )
    }
    for category_key in _SUPPORTED_CATEGORIES
}
# Additive opt-in version. Never change a v1 schema/hash or an existing run.
for _site in ("domestic", "overseas"):
    for _scope in ("whole", "single"):
        _v2 = _contract("knowledge_graph", _site, _scope, "v2")
        _CATALOG_BY_CATEGORY["knowledge_graph"][_v2.contract_key] = _v2
_CATALOG = {
    contract_key: contract
    for catalog in _CATALOG_BY_CATEGORY.values()
    for contract_key, contract in catalog.items()
}

    "kg.domestic.whole.v1": ("domestic", "whole"),
    "kg.domestic.single.v1": ("domestic", "single"),
    "kg.overseas.whole.v1": ("overseas", "whole"),
