def knowledge_graph_contract_catalog(
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> Mapping[str, KnowledgeGraphOutputContract]:
    return MappingProxyType(_CATALOG_BY_CATEGORY.get(category_key, {}))


def get_knowledge_graph_contract(
    contract_key: str,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> KnowledgeGraphOutputContract | None:
    base = _CATALOG.get(contract_key)
    if base is None or snapshot is None or snapshot == base.snapshot():
        return base
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        return None
    try:
        contract = extend_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, snapshot.get("output_schema"))
    except KnowledgeGraphContractError:
        return None
    if snapshot != contract.snapshot():
        return None
    return contract


def extend_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key: str, schema: Any) -> KnowledgeGraphOutputContract:
    """Version schemas inside immutable mechanisms, without a mutable process registry.

    The catalog key identifies the route; contract_hash identifies its exact field
    definition. Existing identity, route and compatibility fields remain intact.
    """
    from dataclasses import replace

    base = _CATALOG.get(contract_key)
    if base is None or not contract_key.startswith("kg.knowledge_graph."):
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_contract_extension_not_allowed", "只开放知识图谱字段定义")
    if len(canonical_json(schema).encode("utf-8")) > 32768:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_too_large", "字段定义不能超过 32 KB")
    count = 0

    def check(value: Any, depth: int = 0) -> None:
        nonlocal count
        count += 1
        if depth > 6 or count > 128 or not isinstance(value, dict):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "字段定义最多 6 层、128 个字段")
        allowed = {"type", "title", "description", "enum", "properties", "required", "additionalProperties", "items"}
        kind = value.get("type")
        if set(value) - allowed or kind not in ("object", "array", "string", "integer", "number", "boolean", "null"):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "字段定义包含不支持的类型或约束；不支持外部引用")
        if any(k in value and (not isinstance(value[k], str) or len(value[k]) > 2000) for k in ("title", "description")):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "字段名称说明必须是 2000 字以内的文本")
        if "enum" in value and (not isinstance(value["enum"], list) or not 1 <= len(value["enum"]) <= 100):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "枚举必须包含 1–100 个值")
        if "enum" in value:
            from .workflow_node_executors import _matches_json_type
            if kind in ("object", "array") or any(not _matches_json_type(v, kind) for v in value["enum"]):
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "枚举值须与字段的标量类型一致")
        if kind == "object":
            if "items" in value:
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "对象不能声明数组 items")
            props = value.get("properties")
            required = value.get("required")
            if (not isinstance(props, dict) or not isinstance(required, list)
                or any(not isinstance(k, str) for k in required)
                or len(required) != len(set(required)) or set(required) != set(props)
                or value.get("additionalProperties") is not False
                or any(not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", k) for k in props)):
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "对象须声明全部字段为 required，并禁止额外字段；字段名使用英文、数字、下划线")
            for child in props.values():
                check(child, depth + 1)
        elif kind == "array":
            if set(value) & {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}:
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "数组不能声明对象字段")
            check(value.get("items"), depth + 1)
        elif set(value) & {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties", "items"}:
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "字段类型与约束不匹配")

    check(schema)
    if schema.get("type") != "object":
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_invalid", "顶层输出必须是对象")
    props = schema["properties"]
    for reserved in {"source", "ll_id", "res_id", "is_single"} - set(base.field_order):
        if reserved in props:
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_protected_field", f"不能新增路线或身份字段 {reserved}")
    for name, old in base.output_schema["properties"].items():
        # Keep all legacy business semantics; new fields are additive.
        if name not in props or knowledge_graph_schema_shape(props[name]) != knowledge_graph_schema_shape(old):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError("kg_schema_protected_field", f"不能删除或改变基础字段 {name} 的规则")
    return replace(base, field_order=(*base.field_order, *sorted(set(props) - set(base.field_order))), _output_schema_json=canonical_json(schema))


def knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(contract_key: str, snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Wrap the authoritative route schema for provider strict output.

    Keeping this adapter beside the route catalog prevents a second field
    registry from drifting away from the existing downstream contract.
    """

    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
    if contract is None:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_contract_unknown",
            f"知识图谱输出合同不存在：{contract_key}",
        )
    route_name = f"kg_{contract.site_scope}_{contract.asset_scope}"
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": route_name,
        "description": "知识图谱标签字段；素材身份与路线字段按冻结合同原样返回",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": contract.output_schema,
    }


def knowledge_graph_fixture_catalog(
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> Mapping[str, str]:
    """Return the server-owned dry-run fixture to output-contract mapping."""

    if category_key not in _CATALOG_BY_CATEGORY:
        return MappingProxyType({})
    return MappingProxyType(
        {
            fixture_key: f"kg.{category_key}.{site_scope}.{asset_scope}.v1"
            for fixture_key, (site_scope, asset_scope) in _FIXTURE_ROUTES.items()
        }
    )


def _schema_fixture(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    # Synthetic fixtures only; never presented as real model inference.
    if "enum" in schema:
        return deepcopy(schema["enum"][0])
    kind = schema["type"]
    if kind == "object":
        return {key: _schema_fixture(value) for key, value in schema["properties"].items()}
    return {"string": "", "integer": 0, "number": 0, "boolean": False, "null": None, "array": []}[kind]


def resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
    fixture_key: str,
    contract_key: str,
    *,
    expected_asset_id: str,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    fixture_route = _FIXTURE_ROUTES.get(fixture_key)
    if fixture_route is None:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_fixture_unknown",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 夹具不存在",
        )
    contract = _require_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
    if fixture_route != (contract.site_scope, contract.asset_scope):
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_fixture_route_mismatch",
            "知识图谱 dry-run 夹具与选中路线不一致",
        )
    payload = deepcopy(_FIXTURE_TEMPLATES[fixture_key])
    payload[contract.identity_field] = expected_asset_id
    payload["is_single"] = contract.is_single
    if "view_angle" in contract.field_order and "" in contract.output_schema["properties"]["view_angle"].get("enum", []):
        # Simulated execution must not pretend to infer an image angle.
        payload["view_angle"] = ""
    ordered = {field: payload[field] if field in payload else _schema_fixture(contract.output_schema["properties"][field]) for field in contract.field_order}
    return validate_knowledge_graph_output(
        contract_key,
        ordered,
        expected_asset_id=expected_asset_id,
        snapshot=snapshot,
    )


def knowledge_graph_contract_summary(
    snapshot: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        return None
    route = snapshot.get("route")
    return {
        key: deepcopy(snapshot[key])
        for key in (
            "schema_version",
            "contract_key",
            "contract_hash",
            "route",
        )
        if key in snapshot and (key != "route" or isinstance(route, Mapping))
    }


def _require_contract(contract_key: str, snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> KnowledgeGraphOutputContract:
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
    if contract is None:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_contract_unknown",
            f"知识图谱输出合同不存在：{contract_key}",
        )
    return contract


def _split_compatibility_values(field: str, raw: str) -> list[str]:
    if not raw:
        return []
    if ", " in raw or " ," in raw:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_value_invalid",
            f"{field} 必须使用无空格的英文逗号连接",
        )
    values = raw.split(",")
    if any(not value for value in values):
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_value_invalid",
            f"{field} 包含空的多值项",
        )
    return values


def _validate_title_value(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    """title 是自由文本标题，不是受控词表字段。

    提示词要求它是 Title Case 的英文短句，正常英文写作就会带逗号加空格。
    按多值字段的逗号规则切它，海外链路会整条报废——run158 十条全灭里有
    九条就是这么死的。这里只拦真正不可用的形态：换行和超长。
    """

    raw = payload.get("title")
    if raw is None:
        return
    text = str(raw)
    if "\n" in text or "\r" in text:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_value_invalid",
            "title 不能包含换行",
        )
    if len(text) > 200:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_value_invalid",
            "title 超过 200 字符",
        )


def _validate_compatibility_values(
    contract: KnowledgeGraphOutputContract,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    _validate_title_value(payload)
    for field in KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS:
        raw = str(payload[field])
        values = _split_compatibility_values(field, raw)
        limit = _MULTI_VALUE_LIMITS.get(field, 1)
        if field == "object" and contract.asset_scope == "single":
            limit = 1
        if field == "style" and contract.asset_scope == "single":
            limit = 1
        if len(values) > limit:
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                "kg_output_value_invalid",
                f"{field} 最多允许 {limit} 个值",
            )
        if field != "object" or not values:
            continue
        if contract.asset_scope == "single":
            if _OBJECT_WEIGHT_PATTERN.fullmatch(values[0]):
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                    "kg_output_value_invalid",
                    "单体链路 object 不能带权重",
                )
            continue
        for index, value in enumerate(values):
            match = _OBJECT_WEIGHT_PATTERN.fullmatch(value)
            if match is None or float(match.group("weight")) != _WHOLE_OBJECT_WEIGHTS[index]:
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                    "kg_output_value_invalid",
                    "整体链路 object 必须按主次使用固定权重 0.7、0.5、0.3",
                )


def shared_route_truth_fields(source_key: str | None, target_key: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Fields an operator may label after a same-site whole/single change."""
    source = get_knowledge_graph_contract(source_key)
    target = get_knowledge_graph_contract(target_key)
    if (source is None or target is None or source_key == target_key
        or source.site_scope != target.site_scope
        or source.contract_key.split(".")[1] != target.contract_key.split(".")[1]):
        return ()
    return tuple(field for field in target.field_order
                 if field in source.field_order and field not in {"ll_id", "res_id", "source", "is_single"})


def validate_knowledge_graph_output(
    contract_key: str,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    expected_asset_id: str,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    contract = _require_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_object_required",
            "知识图谱模型输出必须是 JSON 对象",
        )
    expected = set(contract.field_order)
    actual = set(payload)
    missing = [field for field in contract.field_order if field not in actual]
    if missing:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_fields_missing",
            "知识图谱模型输出缺少字段：" + "、".join(missing),
        )
    unknown = sorted(actual - expected)
    if unknown:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_fields_unknown",
            "知识图谱模型输出包含多余字段：" + "、".join(unknown),
        )
    for field in contract.field_order:
        value = payload[field]
        if field == "is_single":
            if (
                not isinstance(value, int)
                or isinstance(value, bool)
                or value != contract.is_single
            ):
                raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                    "kg_output_route_mismatch",
                    f"{contract.contract_key} 的 is_single 必须为 {contract.is_single}",
                )
        elif field in _require_contract(contract_key).field_order and not isinstance(value, str):
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                "kg_output_field_type_invalid",
                f"{field} 必须是字符串，缺失值使用空字符串",
            )
        allowed = contract.output_schema["properties"][field].get("enum")
        if allowed is not None and value not in allowed:
            raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
                "kg_output_value_invalid", f"{field} 不在该版本允许的取值中",
            )
    from .workflow_node_executors import _validate_output_value, NodeExecutorError
    try:
        _validate_output_value(payload, contract.output_schema)
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    identity = payload[contract.identity_field]
    if not identity or identity != expected_asset_id:
        raise KnowledgeGraphContractError(
            "kg_output_identity_mismatch",
            f"{contract.identity_field} 必须原样返回当前素材 ID",
        )
    _validate_compatibility_values(contract, payload)
    return deepcopy(dict(payload))


def adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output(
    contract_key: str,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    expected_asset_id: str,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    evidence_prefix: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    contract = _require_contract(contract_key, snapshot)
    raw_output = validate_knowledge_graph_output(
        contract_key,
        payload,
        expected_asset_id=expected_asset_id,
        snapshot=snapshot,
    )
    semantic: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    semantic_fields = list(KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS)
    if "view_angle" in contract.field_order and contract.output_schema["properties"]["view_angle"].get("type") == "string":
        semantic_fields.append("view_angle")
    if "title" in contract.field_order:
        semantic_fields.insert(0, "title")
    for field in semantic_fields:
        raw = raw_output[field]
        values = _split_compatibility_values(field, raw)
        if not values:
            continue
        candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for index, encoded in enumerate(values):
            value = encoded
            weight: float | None = None
            if field == "object" and contract.asset_scope == "whole":
                match = _OBJECT_WEIGHT_PATTERN.fullmatch(encoded)
                assert match is not None
                value = match.group("value")
                weight = float(match.group("weight"))
            candidates.append(
                {
                    "value": value,
                    "locale": contract.locale,
                    "rank": index + 1,
                    "weight": weight,
                    "evidence_ref": f"{evidence_prefix}#{field}.{index}",
                }
            )
        semantic[field] = candidates
    return {
        "contract": contract.snapshot(),
        "identity": {
            "field": contract.identity_field,
            "value": raw_output[contract.identity_field],
            "is_single": contract.is_single,
        },
        "semantic": semantic,
        "raw_output": raw_output,
    }


def knowledge_graph_schema_shape(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Compare validation rules, ignoring documentation and set ordering."""
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {key: ({name: knowledge_graph_schema_shape(child) for name, child in item.items()}
                      if key in {"properties", "$defs", "definitions"} and isinstance(item, Mapping)
                      else sorted(item, key=canonical_json) if key in {"required", "enum"} and isinstance(item, list)
                      else knowledge_graph_schema_shape(item))
                for key, item in value.items() if key not in {"title", "description"}}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [knowledge_graph_schema_shape(item) for item in value]
    return value


def knowledge_graph_route_issues(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    """检查机制版本冻结的路线分支条件与字段合同是否还是当前编码。

    海外 is_single 由 0/1 改为 1/2 后，旧版本的分支条件（is_single == 0 选海外整体）
    会把整体素材送进单体提示词、字段合同快照也对不上；必须在发起前拦住，
    并让运营重新生成版本，而不是跑完 400 条才发现。
    """

    issues: list[str] = []
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    edges = [edge for edge in edges if isinstance(edge, Mapping)] if isinstance(edges, list) else []
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = [node for node in nodes if isinstance(node, Mapping)] if isinstance(nodes, list) else []
    for edge in edges:
        branch = edge.get("branch")
        condition = edge.get("condition")
        if not isinstance(branch, str) or not isinstance(condition, Mapping):
            continue
        parts = branch.rsplit("_", 1)
        if len(parts) != 2 or parts[0] not in IS_SINGLE_CODES or parts[1] not in ("whole", "single"):
            continue
        expected = is_single_code(parts[0], parts[1])
        clauses = condition.get("all")
        clauses = [c for c in clauses if isinstance(c, Mapping)] if isinstance(clauses, list) else []
        for clause in clauses:
            if str(clause.get("path") or "").endswith(".is_single") and clause.get("value") != expected:
                issues.append(
                    f"路线 {branch} 的分支条件仍按 is_single={clause.get('value')} 选路，当前编码应为 {expected}"
                )
    for node in nodes:
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        snapshot = config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
        if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
            continue
        contract_key = snapshot.get("contract_key")
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, snapshot) if isinstance(contract_key, str) else None
        if contract is None:
            base = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key) if isinstance(contract_key, str) else None
            if base is not None and snapshot.get("is_single") != base.is_single:
                issues.append(f"节点 {node.get('node_instance_id')} 冻结的字段合同 {contract_key} 已升级（整体/单体编码已变更）")
            else:
                issues.append(f"节点 {node.get('node_instance_id')} 的冻结字段定义无效")
            continue
        explicit = config.get("output_contract")
        if explicit is not None and (
            not isinstance(explicit, Mapping)
            or explicit.get("mode") != "json_schema"
            or knowledge_graph_schema_shape(explicit.get("schema")) != knowledge_graph_schema_shape(contract.output_schema)
        ):
            issues.append(
                f"节点 {node.get('node_instance_id')} 的结构化输出与字段版本 {contract_key} 不一致；请重新选择字段版本并生成新机制"
            )
        current = knowledge_graph_contract_summary(contract.snapshot())
        frozen_hash = snapshot.get("contract_hash")
        if current and frozen_hash != current.get("contract_hash"):
            issues.append(
                f"节点 {node.get('node_instance_id')} 冻结的字段合同 {contract_key} 已升级（海外 is_single 改为 1/2）"
            )
        # 提示词正文若仍要求模型输出旧编码（kg-native-v2 曾复用旧海外提示词），
        # 模型会照抄旧值、逐条被合同拒绝；这里按正文里的显式数字提前拦住。
        stated = _prompt_stated_is_single(config)
        if stated is not None and stated != contract.is_single:
            issues.append(
                f"节点 {node.get('node_instance_id')} 的提示词正文仍要求输出 is_single={stated}，当前合同为 {contract.is_single}"
            )
    return issues


_PROMPT_IS_SINGLE_PATTERNS = (
    re.compile(r"is_single[^●\n]{0,40}?number (\d)"),
    re.compile(r"is_single[^●\n]{0,20}?固定为数字 (\d)"),
    re.compile(r'"is_single":\s*(\d)'),
)


def _prompt_stated_is_single(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int | None:
    """从冻结提示词正文里读出它要求模型输出的 is_single 值；读不出返回 None。"""

    component = config.get("component_snapshot")
    component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
    content = component.get("content")
    if isinstance(content, str):
        try:
            content = json.loads(content)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return None
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        return None
    text = "\n".join(
        str(content.get(key) or "") for key in ("system_prompt", "user_prompt")
    )
    values = {
        int(match.group(1))
        for pattern in _PROMPT_IS_SINGLE_PATTERNS
        for match in pattern.finditer(text)
    }
    if len(values) != 1:
        return None
    return values.pop()

    "overseas_whole": "You are a high-precision tag extractor for environmental art, interior, architecture, and landscape 3D/SU model images. The current route is \"overseas whole model\". Analyze only the single image in the current request and never use content from previous calls.Precision is more important than recall. Extract only clear, major, directly visible information. Use \"\" for absent or uncertain values; never use null, [], \"unknown\", or \"none\".Allowed controlled vocabularies for this call:●space: living room,dining
