def _strict_object(
    properties: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    required: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": deepcopy(dict(properties)),
        "required": list(required),
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


def _foreach_output_schema(item_output_schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    error_schema = _closed(
        {
            "code": {"type": "string"},
            "message": {"type": "string"},
        }
    )
    item_properties = {
        "item_key": {"type": "string"},
        "ordinal": {"type": "integer"},
        "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["succeeded", "failed", "skipped"],
        },
        "attempt_count": {"type": "integer"},
        "input_hash": {"type": "string"},
        "output_hash": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
        "output": deepcopy(dict(item_output_schema)),
        "error": error_schema,
    }
    return _closed(
        {
            "schema_version": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["workflow-foreach-output-v1"],
            },
            "node_instance_id": {"type": "string"},
            "source_hash": {"type": "string"},
            "item_contract_hash": {"type": "string"},
            "counts": _closed(
                {
                    "total": {"type": "integer"},
                    "succeeded": {"type": "integer"},
                    "failed": {"type": "integer"},
                    "skipped": {"type": "integer"},
                }
            ),
            "items": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": _strict_object(
                    item_properties,
                    required=(
                        "item_key",
                        "ordinal",
                        "status",
                        "attempt_count",
                        "input_hash",
                    ),
                ),
            },
        }
    )


def _aggregate_output_schema(
    item_output_schema: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    feature_names: tuple[str, ...],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    scalar = {"type": ["string", "number", "boolean", "null"]}
    feature_value = {
        "type": ["string", "number", "boolean", "array", "null"],
        "items": deepcopy(scalar),
    }
    feature_summary = _closed(
        {
            "observed_items": {"type": "integer"},
            "missing_items": {"type": "integer"},
            "values": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": _closed(
                    {
                        "value": deepcopy(scalar),
                        "count": {"type": "integer"},
                    }
                ),
            },
        }
    )
    base_item = {
        "item_key": {"type": "string"},
        "ordinal": {"type": "integer"},
        "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["succeeded", "failed", "skipped"],
        },
        "attempt_count": {"type": "integer"},
        "input_hash": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
    }
    result_item = _strict_object(
        {
            **base_item,
            "output_hash": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
            "score": {"type": "number"},
            "features": _closed(
                {name: deepcopy(feature_value) for name in feature_names}
            ),
            "output": deepcopy(dict(item_output_schema)),
        },
        required=(
            *base_item,
            "output_hash",
            "score",
            "features",
            "output",
        ),
    )
    failure_item = _closed(
        {
            **base_item,
            "error": _closed(
                {
                    "code": {"type": "string"},
                    "message": {"type": "string"},
                }
            ),
        }
    )
    return _closed(
        {
            "schema_version": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["workflow-case-aggregate-v1"],
            },
            "foreach_node_id": {"type": "string"},
            "source_hash": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
            "item_contract_hash": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
            "counts": _closed(
                {
                    "source_total": {"type": "integer"},
                    "included": {"type": "integer"},
                    "succeeded": {"type": "integer"},
                    "failed": {"type": "integer"},
                    "skipped": {"type": "integer"},
                }
            ),
            "score": _closed(
                {
                    "count": {"type": "integer"},
                    "mean": {"type": ["number", "null"]},
                    "minimum": {"type": ["number", "null"]},
                    "maximum": {"type": ["number", "null"]},
                    "distribution": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "additionalProperties": {"type": "integer"},
                    },
                }
            ),
            "features": _closed(
                {name: deepcopy(feature_summary) for name in feature_names}
            ),
            "results": {"type": "array", "items": result_item},
            "failures": {"type": "array", "items": failure_item},
        }
    )


def _node(
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    output_schema: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "config": deepcopy(dict(config)),
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": deepcopy(dict(output_schema or {"type": "object"})),
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0},
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "trace_id"],
    }


def _ensure_profile(db: Session, *, actor: str) -> EvaluationCategoryProfile:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "design_case"
        )
    )
    expected = {
        "display_name": "设计案例",
        "status": "active",
        "allowed_mime_types_json": canonical_json(
            ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif"]
        ),
        "preprocess_config_json": canonical_json(
            {"preprocess": "image", "case_collection": True}
        ),
        "pipeline_config_json": canonical_json(default_pipeline("design_case")),
    }
    if row is None:
        row = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key="design_case",
            description="案例逐图评测、聚合与素材综合评审",
            created_by=actor,
            **expected,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        return row
    actual = {key: getattr(row, key) for key in expected}
    if actual != expected:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_category_conflict",
            "design_case 类目已存在但与冻结候选合同不一致",
        )
    return row


def _ensure_asset(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    asset_key: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    actor: str,
    module_type_key: str | None = None,
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )
    expected = {
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "module_type_key": module_type_key,
        "status": "active",
    }
    if row is None:
        row = WorkflowNodeAsset(
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key=asset_key,
            description="隔离候选工作流专用资产",
            is_discoverable=True,
            created_by=actor,
            **expected,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        return row
    if {key: getattr(row, key) for key in expected} != expected:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_asset_conflict",
            f"资产 {category_key}/{asset_key} 已存在但内容漂移",
        )
    return row


def _ensure_working_copy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    actor: str,
    base_prompt_version_id: int | None = None,
    base_rule_module_version_id: int | None = None,
) -> EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy:
    """Make release-owned components immediately editable without overwriting operators.

    The isolated release used to create immutable prompt/rule versions directly.
    That was executable, but the operator component page requires a working-copy row
    and otherwise returned 404 forever. Existing working copies are intentionally
    preserved verbatim so a repeat release can never replace operator edits.
    """

    row = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
        )
    )
    if row is not None:
        return row
    editor_schema_version = (
        "prompt-component-editor-v1"
        if asset.kind == "prompt"
        else require_module_type(
            str(asset.module_type_key or "")
        ).editor_schema_version
    )
    row = EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        base_prompt_version_id=base_prompt_version_id,
        base_rule_module_version_id=base_rule_module_version_id,
        content_json=canonical_json(content),
        editor_schema_version=editor_schema_version,
        lock_version=1,
        updated_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _ensure_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    asset_key: str,
    stage: str,
    version: str,
    name: str,
    system_prompt: str,
    actor: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    asset = _ensure_asset(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        asset_key=asset_key,
        kind="prompt",
        display_name=name,
        actor=actor,
    )
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion).where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == stage,
            PromptVersion.version == version,
        )
    ).all()
    expected = {
        "pipeline_scope": "shared",
        "name": name,
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": "",
        "rubric_version": "legacy-native-v1",
        "status": "draft",
        "source": "legacy_release",
    }
    if len(rows) > 1:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_prompt_duplicate", f"提示词版本重复：{version}"
        )
    if rows:
        row = rows[0]
        if {key: getattr(row, key) for key in expected} != expected:
            raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
                "legacy_release_prompt_conflict",
                f"提示词版本已存在但内容漂移：{version}",
            )
    else:
        row = PromptVersion(
            category_key=category_key,
            stage=stage,
            version=version,
            change_note="隔离候选；默认关闭思考；不激活",
            created_by=actor,
            **expected,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    bind_prompt_version(db, asset=asset, prompt_version=row, created_by=actor)
    _ensure_working_copy(
        db,
        asset=asset,
        content={
            "stage": row.stage,
            "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": row.user_prompt,
            "rubric_version": row.rubric_version,
        },
        actor=actor,
        base_prompt_version_id=row.id,
    )
    return row


def _ensure_case_score_rule(db: Session, *, actor: str) -> tuple[WorkflowNodeAsset, RuleModuleVersion]:
    asset = _ensure_asset(
        db,
        category_key="design_case",
        asset_key="case_final_score",
        kind="rule_module",
        display_name="案例最终分与过滤建议",
        module_type_key="score_level_matcher",
        actor=actor,
    )
    content = validate_module_content(
        "score_level_matcher",
        {
            "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
            "levels": [
                {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
                {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
                {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
                {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 20},
                {"level": "L5", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
            ],
            "case_score_formula": {
                "schema_version": "case-final-score-v1",
                "model_score_field": "score",
                "quality_score_field": "quality_score",
                "time_score_field": "time_score",
                "threshold_field": "filter_threshold",
                "comparison": "strict_gt",
                "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
            },
        },
    )
    content_hash = canonical_component_hash(content)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
            RuleModuleVersion.content_hash == content_hash,
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        latest_version = db.scalar(
            select(func.max(RuleModuleVersion.version)).where(
                RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id
            )
        )
        row = RuleModuleVersion(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
            module_type_key="score_level_matcher",
            content_json=canonical_json(content),
            content_hash=content_hash,
            source_working_copy_revision=1,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    elif row.content_json != canonical_json(content):
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_rule_conflict", "案例最终评分规则内容漂移"
        )
    bind_rule_module_version(
        db, asset=asset, rule_module_version=row, created_by=actor
    )
    _ensure_working_copy(
        db,
        asset=asset,
        content=content,
        actor=actor,
        base_rule_module_version_id=row.id,
    )
    return asset, row


def _prompt_config(
    base: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    asset_key: str,
    prompt_id: int,
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    modality: str,
    vision_policy: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    config = deepcopy(dict(base))
    for key in (
        "component_snapshot",
        "model_snapshot",
        "vision_capability_snapshot",
        "vision_policy_hash",
        "output_contract_snapshot",
        "short_circuit_rules",
        "execution_role",
    ):
        config.pop(key, None)
    config.update(
        {
            "asset_key": asset_key,
            "prompt_version_id": prompt_id,
            "input_modality": modality,
            "thinking_level": "none",
            "thinking_enabled": False,
            "structured_output": True,
            "output_contract": deepcopy(dict(contract)),
        }
    )
    if vision_policy is None:
        config.pop("vision_input_policy", None)
    else:
        config["vision_input_policy"] = deepcopy(dict(vision_policy))
    return config


def _base_manifest(strict_workflow: WorkflowVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(strict_workflow)
    for node in manifest["nodes"]:
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            continue
        if node["kind"] == "prompt":
            config["thinking_level"] = "none"
            config["thinking_enabled"] = False
    return manifest


# 模型调用节点给一次自动重试：供应商偶发的非法 JSON / 瞬时错误被系统判为
# retryable，却因来源工作流（native 迁移默认）max_attempts=1 从未真正重试过
# （137 run 5019 实证）。提示词节点是从来源清单深拷贝来的，所以在最终清单上统一覆盖；
# 规则节点是确定性计算，保持来源策略。
PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY: dict[str, Any] = {
    "kind": "fixed",
    "max_attempts": 2,
    "delay_seconds": 5,
}


def _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        # foreach_image 的逐图 attempt 上限也读节点 retry_policy（workflow_foreach_executor._max_attempts），
        # 案例链单图 schema 失配同样需要一次重试才能自愈。
        if isinstance(node, dict) and node.get("kind") in {"prompt", "foreach_image"}:
            node["retry_policy"] = deepcopy(PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY)
    return manifest


def _latest_ab_manifest(base: Mapping[str, Any], *, fingerprint: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = deepcopy(dict(base))
    policy = manifest.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
    policy.update(
        {
            "activated": False,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            "isolated_comparison": "ab",
            "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
            "source_provenance": deepcopy(_SOURCE_PROVENANCE),
        }
    )
    return _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)


def _latest_single_manifest(
    db: Session,
    base: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    prompt: PromptVersion,
    fingerprint: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = deepcopy(dict(base))
    prompt_nodes = [node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"]
    if len(prompt_nodes) != 2:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_latest_topology_invalid",
            "最新灵感图来源不是固定 A/B 双提示词拓扑",
        )
    prompt_a = next((node for node in prompt_nodes if node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"), None)
    prompt_b = next((node for node in prompt_nodes if node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_b"), None)
    if prompt_a is None or prompt_b is None:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_latest_topology_invalid", "最新灵感图缺少 prompt_a/prompt_b"
        )
    merged = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [
            prompt_a["config"]["output_contract"],
            prompt_b["config"]["output_contract"],
        ],
        name="inspiration_latest_single",
        description="灵感图最新 A/B 职责单调用严格输出",
    )
    single = deepcopy(prompt_a)
    single["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_single"
    single["display_name"] = "最新机制单提示词"
    single["config"] = _prompt_config(
        prompt_a["config"],
        asset_key="latest_single_prompt",
        prompt_id=prompt.id,
        contract=merged,
        modality="image",
        vision_policy=STRICT_HIGH_POLICY,
    )
    single["config"]["execution_role"] = "classification_and_grading"
    single["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = deepcopy(
        prompt_a["config"].get("short_circuit_rules", [])
    )
    single["output_schema"] = deepcopy(merged["schema"])
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        single if node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a" else node
        for node in manifest["nodes"]
        if node["node_instance_id"] != "prompt_b"
    ]
    _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)

    edges: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str | None]] = set()
    for edge in manifest["edges"]:
        source = "prompt_single" if edge["from"] == "prompt_a" else edge["from"]
        target = "prompt_single" if edge["to"] == "prompt_a" else edge["to"]
        if source == "prompt_b":
            continue
        if target == "prompt_b":
            target = "media_adjustment_3"
        key = (source, target, edge.get("branch"))
        if source != target and key not in seen:
            edges.append({**edge, "from": source, "to": target})
            seen.add(key)
    manifest["edges"] = edges
    layout = manifest.get("layout")
    if isinstance(layout, dict):
        layout.pop("prompt_b", None)
        layout["prompt_single"] = layout.pop("prompt_a", {"x": 324, "y": 96})
    policy = manifest.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
    policy.update(
        {
            "activated": False,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            "isolated_comparison": "single",
            "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
            "source_provenance": deepcopy(_SOURCE_PROVENANCE),
        }
    )
    return manifest


def _legacy_inspiration_manifest(
    base: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    prompt: PromptVersion,
    fingerprint: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    source_prompt = next(node for node in base["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    contract = _legacy_inspiration_contract()
    prompt_config = _prompt_config(
        source_prompt["config"],
        asset_key="legacy_doubao_single_prompt",
        prompt_id=prompt.id,
        contract=contract,
        modality="image",
        vision_policy=LEGACY_SHORT_EDGE_POLICY,
    )
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取旧链路输入",
            {"connector_key": "logical.inspiration_image.legacy_input", "input_mode": "initial_intake"},
        ),
        _node(
            "prompt_single",
            "prompt",
            "旧豆包单提示词",
            prompt_config,
            output_schema=contract["schema"],
        ),
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "输出旧链路审核建议",
            {
                "connector_key": "logical.inspiration_image.legacy_output",
                "completion_mode": "continue",
            },
        ),
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "lane_key": "legacy_doubao_review",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_single"},
            {"from": "prompt_single", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {
            "max_parallel": 1,
            "activated": False,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
            "legacy_semantics": "documented_php_chain",
        },
        "layout": {
            "upstream": {"x": 64, "y": 96},
            "prompt_single": {"x": 324, "y": 96},
            "downstream": {"x": 584, "y": 96},
        },
    }
    return _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)


def _legacy_3d_su_manifest(
    base: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    variant: str,
    prompt: PromptVersion,
    fingerprint: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """单提示词 + 图片原样上传 + 严格 JSON，与 3d66.new-cli ai:resLLM 请求形状一致。"""

    spec = LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS[variant]
    source_prompt = next(node for node in base["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    contract = _legacy_3d_su_contract(variant)
    prompt_config = _prompt_config(
        source_prompt["config"],
        asset_key=spec["asset_key"],
        prompt_id=prompt.id,
        contract=contract,
        modality="image",
        vision_policy=LEGACY_3D_SU_ORIGINAL_POLICY,
    )
    label = "3D(Max)" if variant == "max" else "SU"
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            f"读取 {label} 旧链路输入",
            {"connector_key": "logical.model_3d_su.legacy_input", "input_mode": "initial_intake"},
        ),
        _node(
            "prompt_single",
            "prompt",
            f"{label} 旧豆包单提示词",
            prompt_config,
            output_schema=contract["schema"],
        ),
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            f"输出 {label} 旧链路评级结果",
            {
                "connector_key": "logical.model_3d_su.legacy_output",
                "completion_mode": "continue",
            },
        ),
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "model_3d_su",
        "lane_key": f"legacy_doubao_{variant}",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_single"},
            {"from": "prompt_single", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {
            "max_parallel": 1,
            "activated": False,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
            "legacy_semantics": "documented_php_chain",
            "legacy_source": {
                "system": "3d66.new-cli ai:resLLM",
                "config_table": "master_3d66_main.ll_llm_app_config",
                "config_id": spec["source_config_id"],
                "res_type": spec["res_type"],
                "reasoning_effort": "minimal",
                "response_format_name": "math_reasoning",
                "result_table": (
                    "master_3d66_main.ll_max_doubao_model_result_log"
                    if variant == "max"
                    else "master_3d66_main.ll_su_doubao_model_result_log"
                ),
            },
        },
        "layout": {
            "upstream": {"x": 64, "y": 96},
            "prompt_single": {"x": 324, "y": 96},
            "downstream": {"x": 584, "y": 96},
        },
    }
    return _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)


def _case_manifest(
    base: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    image_prompt: PromptVersion,
    text_prompt: PromptVersion,
    score_asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    score_rule: RuleModuleVersion,
    fingerprint: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    source_prompt = next(node for node in base["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    image_contract = _case_image_contract()
    text_contract = _case_text_contract()
    image_config = _prompt_config(
        source_prompt["config"],
        asset_key="case_image_review",
        prompt_id=image_prompt.id,
        contract=image_contract,
        modality="image",
        vision_policy=STRICT_HIGH_POLICY,
    )
    image_config["kind"] = "prompt"
    text_config = _prompt_config(
        source_prompt["config"],
        asset_key="case_text_review",
        prompt_id=text_prompt.id,
        contract=text_contract,
        modality="text",
        vision_policy=None,
    )
    readonly_case_review = deepcopy(text_contract["schema"])
    readonly_case_review["x-correction-editable"] = False
    score_output = _closed(
        {
            "score": {"type": "number"},
            "level": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
            },
            "filter_status": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["pass", "filter"],
            },
            "reason": {"type": "string"},
            "model_score": {"type": "number"},
            "quality_score": {"type": "number"},
            "time_score": {"type": "number"},
            "final_score": {"type": "number"},
            "filter_threshold": {"type": "number"},
            "filter_result": {"type": "integer", "enum": [1, 2]},
            "delete_recommendation": {"type": "boolean"},
            "recommended_image_ids": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
            },
            # The downstream node receives only its direct predecessor. Carry
            # the exact prompt result forward in the closed score envelope,
            # but mark it read-only so correction discovery keeps the prompt
            # node as the sole editable fact source.
            "case_review": readonly_case_review,
            "side_effect_executed": {"type": "boolean", "const": False},
            "deletion_mode": {"type": "string", "const": "recommend_only"},
        }
    )
    normalized_case_output = _closed(
        {
            "schema_version": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["design-case-evaluation-result-v1"],
            },
            "case_cloud_id": {"type": "string"},
            **deepcopy(score_output["properties"]),
            "case_review": deepcopy(text_contract["schema"]),
        }
    )
    downstream_output = _closed(
        {
            "schema_version": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["case-recommendation-projection-v1"],
            },
            "source_node_ids": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "normalized_result": normalized_case_output,
            "side_effect_executed": {"type": "boolean", "const": False},
            "deletion_mode": {"type": "string", "const": "recommend_only"},
            "external_io_performed": {"type": "boolean", "const": False},
        }
    )
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "case_source",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取案例及全部图片",
            {"connector_key": "logical.design_case.input", "input_mode": "initial_intake"},
        ),
        _node(
            "foreach_images",
            "foreach_image",
            "逐图片严格评测",
            {
                # Downstream nodes receive canonical predecessor envelopes. The
                # connector keeps the frozen business payload under `output`,
                # so the release path must match the runtime shape instead of
                # reading the production-run root input directly.
                "items_path": "$.upstream.case_source.output.case.images",
                "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                "max_items": 10000,
                "max_parallel_items": 8,
                "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                "item_executor": image_config,
            },
            output_schema=_foreach_output_schema(image_contract["schema"]),
        ),
        _node(
            "aggregate_images",
            "aggregate_case",
            "聚合案例图片结果",
            {
                "foreach_node_id": "foreach_images",
                "included_statuses": ["succeeded"],
                "score_path": "$.score",
                "feature_paths": {
                    "trait": "$.trait",
                    "category": "$.category",
                    "style": "$.style",
                },
                "score_buckets": [
                    {"label": "0-59", "min_inclusive": 0, "max_exclusive": 60},
                    {"label": "60-79", "min_inclusive": 60, "max_exclusive": 80},
                    {"label": "80-101", "min_inclusive": 80, "max_exclusive": 101},
                ],
                "empty_policy": "emit_empty",
                "failure_projection": "include",
                # 素材（案例）维度：终态结果带上图片总数、分数分布与各特征计数，
                # 供素材维度基准集逐项回归。聚合节点自身输出不变。
                "emit_material_summary": True,
            },
            output_schema=_aggregate_output_schema(
                image_contract["schema"],
                feature_names=("trait", "category", "style"),
            ),
        ),
        _node(
            "case_text_prompt",
            "prompt",
            "案例素材综合评审",
            text_config,
            output_schema=text_contract["schema"],
        ),
        _node(
            "case_final_score",
            "rule_module",
            "最终分与过滤建议",
            {
                "asset_key": score_asset.asset_key,
                "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
                "rule_module_version_id": score_rule.id,
                "input_bindings": {
                    "model_score": "case_text_prompt.output.score",
                    "quality_score": "case_source.output.case.quality_score",
                    "time_score": "case_source.output.case.time_score",
                    "filter_threshold": "case_source.output.case.filter_threshold",
                },
                "scope": "design_case_legacy_native_v1",
                "execution_role": "level_matching",
            },
            output_schema=score_output,
        ),
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "输出案例评审与删图建议",
            {
                "connector_key": "logical.design_case.output",
                "completion_mode": "continue",
                "mapping": {
                    "schema_version": "case-recommendation-projection-v1",
                    "case_review_node_id": "case_text_prompt",
                    "final_score_node_id": "case_final_score",
                    "side_effect_executed": False,
                    "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
                },
            },
            output_schema=downstream_output,
        ),
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "design_case",
        "lane_key": "legacy_doubao_review",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": "case_source", "to": "foreach_images"},
            {"from": "foreach_images", "to": "aggregate_images"},
            {"from": "aggregate_images", "to": "case_text_prompt"},
            {"from": "case_text_prompt", "to": "case_final_score"},
            {"from": "case_final_score", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "content_key": {"type": "string"},
                "asset_id": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
                "asset_sha256": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
                },
                "baseline_set_item_id": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
                "expected_result": {"type": "object"},
                "truth_source": {"type": "string"},
                "truth_revision": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0},
                "truth_revision_id": {"type": ["integer", "null"]},
                "truth_hash": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
                },
                "workflow_fixture": {"type": "object"},
                "case": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "cloud_id": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                        "project_id": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                        "case_name": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                        "case_text": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                        "quality_score": {"type": "number"},
                        "time_score": {"type": "number"},
                        "filter_threshold": {"type": "number"},
                        "parent_class_name": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "class_name": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "own_keyword": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "style_tag": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "origin_publish_time": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "designer_company": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "design_highlights": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "other_important_info": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "country": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "scale": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "dtime": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "btime": {"type": ["string", "integer", "number"]},
                        "images": {
                            "type": "array",
                            "items": {
                                "type": "object",
                                "properties": {
                                    "cloud_id": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                                    "oss_url": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                                    "asset_id": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
                                    "asset_sha256": {
                                        "type": "string",
                                        "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
                                    },
                                },
                                "required": [
                                    "cloud_id",
                                    "oss_url",
                                    "asset_id",
                                    "asset_sha256",
                                ],
                                "additionalProperties": False,
                            },
                            "minItems": 1,
                            "maxItems": 10000,
                        },
                    },
                    "required": [
                        "cloud_id",
                        "project_id",
                        "case_name",
                        "case_text",
                        "quality_score",
                        "time_score",
                        "filter_threshold",
                        "images",
                    ],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                }
            },
            "required": ["asset_id", "asset_sha256", "case"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {
            "max_parallel": 8,
            "activated": False,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            "physical_delete_enabled": False,
            "delete_behavior": "recommend_only",
            "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
        },
        "layout": {
            "case_source": {"x": 64, "y": 96},
            "foreach_images": {"x": 324, "y": 96},
            "aggregate_images": {"x": 584, "y": 96},
            "case_text_prompt": {"x": 844, "y": 96},
            "case_final_score": {"x": 1104, "y": 96},
            "downstream": {"x": 1364, "y": 96},
        },
    }
    return _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)


def _ensure_definition(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_key: str,
    name: str,
    category_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowDefinition:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key)
    )
    expected = {
        "name": name,
        "description": "隔离候选工作流；默认不激活；仅用于回归与运营验收",
        "owner": "evaluation-platform",
        "allowed_categories_json": canonical_json([category_key]),
        "status": "active",
    }
    if row is None:
        return create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            name=name,
            description=expected["description"],
            owner=expected["owner"],
            allowed_categories=[category_key],
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if {key: getattr(row, key) for key in expected} != expected:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_definition_conflict",
            f"工作流定义 {workflow_key} 已存在但内容漂移",
        )
    return row


def _ensure_workflow(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_key: str,
    name: str,
    category_key: str,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    fingerprint: str,
    actor: str,
) -> tuple[WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion]:
    definition = _ensure_definition(
        db,
        workflow_key=workflow_key,
        name=name,
        category_key=category_key,
        actor=actor,
    )
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    assert_native_workflow_executable(frozen)
    version = f"v1-20260901-{fingerprint[:12]}"
    expected_hash = _hash(frozen)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == version,
        )
    )
    created = row is None
    if row is None:
        row = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version=version,
            manifest=frozen,
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if row.status != "draft" or row.canonical_hash != expected_hash:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_version_conflict",
            f"候选工作流 {workflow_key}/{version} 已存在但内容或状态漂移",
        )
    if created:
        row.validation_report_json = canonical_json(
            {
                "ok": True,
                "errors": [],
                "candidate": True,
                "activated": False,
                "source_fingerprint": fingerprint,
                "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
            }
        )
        db.flush()
    return definition, row


def _preview_items(strict_plan_hash: str, *, model_id: str | None = None) -> tuple[LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem, ...]:
    prompt_sources = {
        "legacy": _prompt_text("inspiration_legacy_single_v1.txt"),
        "latest_single": _prompt_text("inspiration_latest_single_v2.txt"),
        "case_image": _prompt_text("design_case_image_review_v1.txt"),
        "case_text": _prompt_text("design_case_text_review_v1.txt"),
    }
    fingerprints = {
        "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review": _hash(
            {
                "strict_plan_hash": strict_plan_hash,
                "prompt": prompt_sources["legacy"],
                "contract": _legacy_inspiration_contract(),
                "vision_input_policy": LEGACY_SHORT_EDGE_POLICY,
            }
        ),
        "inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison": _hash(
            {"strict_plan_hash": strict_plan_hash, "mode": "ab", "provenance": _SOURCE_PROVENANCE}
        ),
        "inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison": _hash(
            {
                "strict_plan_hash": strict_plan_hash,
                "mode": "single",
                "prompt": prompt_sources["latest_single"],
                "provenance": _SOURCE_PROVENANCE,
            }
        ),
        "design_case.legacy_doubao_review": _hash(
            {
                "image_prompt": prompt_sources["case_image"],
                "text_prompt": prompt_sources["case_text"],
                "image_contract": _case_image_contract(),
                "text_contract": _case_text_contract(),
                "strict_plan_hash": strict_plan_hash,
                "vision_input_policy": STRICT_HIGH_POLICY,
                "runtime": "foreach_image+aggregate_case+material_summary+case-final-score-v1",
            }
        ),
    }
    for variant, spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.items():
        # 3D/SU 候选只依赖线上提示词与合同，不随灵感图严格候选变化。
        fingerprints[spec["workflow_key"]] = _hash(
            {
                "prompt": _prompt_text(spec["prompt_file"]),
                "contract": _legacy_3d_su_contract(variant),
                "vision_input_policy": LEGACY_3D_SU_ORIGINAL_POLICY,
                "legacy_source": {"config_id": spec["source_config_id"], "res_type": spec["res_type"]},
                **({"model_id": model_id} if model_id else {}),
            }
        )
    return tuple(
        LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem(
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            category_key=category_key,
            version=f"v1-20260901-{fingerprints[workflow_key][:12]}",
            source_fingerprint=fingerprints[workflow_key],
            status="draft",
            workflow_definition_id=None,
            workflow_version_id=None,
            canonical_hash=None,
        )
        for workflow_key, _name, category_key in _RELEASE_SPECS
    )


LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS = tuple(spec["workflow_key"] for spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.values())


def _legacy_inspiration_base_workflow(db: Session) -> WorkflowVersion | None:
    """已物化的灵感图旧链路最新版本：单提示词拓扑，可作为 3D/SU 候选的基座。"""

    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"
        )
    )
    if definition is None:
        return None
    return db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion)
        .where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id)
        .order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _resolve_model_registry(db: Session, model_id: str | None) -> tuple[int | None, str | None]:
    """按模型 ID 找可冻结的 main/benchmark 登记项（优先 main）；返回 (registry_id, model_id)。

    ``model_id`` 也可以写成 ``registry:<id>`` 直接指定登记项。同一模型可能有多条登记，
    其中计价合同损坏的条目会让 ``freeze_component_references`` 失败，这里预先用
    ``build_model_config_snapshot`` 试冻结并跳过不可用的条目。
    """

    if not model_id:
        return None, None
    from .models import ModelConfig
    from .strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot

    if model_id.startswith("registry:"):
        raw = model_id.split(":", 1)[1]
        row = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, int(raw)) if raw.isdigit() else None
        rows = [row] if row is not None and row.active else []
    else:
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(
                ModelRegistryEntry.model_id == model_id,
                ModelRegistryEntry.active.is_(True),
                ModelRegistryEntry.role.in_(("main", "benchmark")),
            )
        ).all()
        rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda row: (0 if row.role == "main" else 1, row.id))
    if not rows:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_model_unavailable", f"模型登记中没有生效的 {model_id}"
        )
    problems: list[str] = []
    for row in rows:
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, row.source_model_config_id) if row.source_model_config_id else None
        if config is None or not config.active:
            problems.append(f"登记 {row.id} 缺少可用的模型配置")
            continue
        try:
            build_model_config_snapshot(config)
        except Exception as exc:  # 计价合同/能力档案损坏等，逐条跳过
            problems.append(f"登记 {row.id}（配置 {config.id}）无法冻结：{exc}")
            continue
        return row.id, row.model_id
    raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
        "legacy_release_model_unfreezable",
        f"{model_id} 的登记项都无法冻结进工作流：" + "；".join(problems),
    )


def _materialize_3d_su_only(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    apply: bool,
    model_id: str | None = None,
) -> LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt:
    """只物化 3D(Max)/SU 两条候选，不触碰灵感图严格候选。

    运营可能已把灵感图评测工作流切到 LEGACY 阶段提示词，此时严格候选发布会
    拒绝取源；3D/SU 候选只需要一个带模型登记的提示词节点配置做基座，优先复用
    已物化的灵感图旧链路版本，其次才尝试严格候选。
    """

    preview = tuple(
        item for item in _preview_items("", model_id=model_id)
        if item.workflow_key in LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS
    )
    registry_id, _resolved_model = _resolve_model_registry(db, model_id) if apply else (None, None)
    if not apply:
        return LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt(
            schema_version="legacy-workflow-release-receipt-v1",
            mode="preview",
            workflows=preview,
        )
    base_workflow = _legacy_inspiration_base_workflow(db)
    if base_workflow is None:
        strict = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
            db, actor=actor, apply=True, category_keys=("inspiration_image",)
        )
        receipt = next(item for item in strict.workflows if item.category_key == "inspiration_image")
        base_workflow = (
            db.get(WorkflowVersion, receipt.candidate_workflow_id)
            if receipt.candidate_workflow_id is not None
            else None
        )
    if base_workflow is None:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_strict_source_missing", "缺少可作为 3D/SU 候选基座的灵感图工作流版本"
        )
    base = _base_manifest(base_workflow)
    by_key = {item.workflow_key: item for item in preview}
    applied: list[LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem] = []
    for variant, spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.items():
        planned = by_key[spec["workflow_key"]]
        prompt = _ensure_prompt(
            db,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            asset_key=spec["asset_key"],
            stage=spec["stage"],
            version=f"legacy-3d-su-{variant}-{planned.source_fingerprint[:12]}",
            name=spec["prompt_name"],
            system_prompt=_prompt_text(spec["prompt_file"]),
            actor=actor,
        )
        name = next(item[1] for item in _RELEASE_SPECS if item[0] == spec["workflow_key"])
        manifest = _legacy_3d_su_manifest(
            base, variant=variant, prompt=prompt, fingerprint=planned.source_fingerprint
        )
        if registry_id is not None:
            for node in manifest["nodes"]:
                if node["kind"] == "prompt":
                    node["config"]["model_registry_id"] = registry_id
            manifest["resource_policy"]["legacy_source"]["model_override"] = model_id
        definition, version = _ensure_workflow(
            db,
            workflow_key=spec["workflow_key"],
            name=name,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            manifest=manifest,
            fingerprint=planned.source_fingerprint,
            actor=actor,
        )
        applied.append(
            LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem(
                workflow_key=spec["workflow_key"],
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                version=version.version,
                source_fingerprint=planned.source_fingerprint,
                status=version.status,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                canonical_hash=version.canonical_hash,
            )
        )
    return LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt(
        schema_version="legacy-workflow-release-receipt-v1",
        mode="apply",
        workflows=tuple(applied),
    )


def materialize_legacy_workflows(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    apply: bool = False,
    workflow_keys: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    model_id: str | None = None,
) -> LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt:
    normalized_actor = actor.strip() if isinstance(actor, str) else ""
    if apply and (not normalized_actor or len(normalized_actor) > 80):
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_actor_required", "应用候选时必须提供 1 至 80 字符审计 actor"
        )
    if workflow_keys is not None:
        selected = tuple(dict.fromkeys(workflow_keys))
        unknown = [key for key in selected if key not in {spec[0] for spec in _RELEASE_SPECS}]
        if unknown or not selected:
            raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
                "legacy_release_workflow_unknown", "指定的工作流不在旧链路候选清单中"
            )
        if set(selected) <= set(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS):
            return _materialize_3d_su_only(
                db, actor=normalized_actor or "system:legacy-release-preview", apply=apply, model_id=model_id
            )
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_workflow_subset_unsupported",
            "只支持单独物化 3D/SU 候选；其余候选请整体发布",
        )

    if model_id:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_model_override_unsupported", "模型覆盖只支持单独物化 3D/SU 候选"
        )
    # 旧机制发布只消费灵感图候选；不连带物化其它类目，避免其漂移阻断本发布。
    strict = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
        db,
        actor=normalized_actor or "system:legacy-release-preview",
        apply=apply,
        category_keys=("inspiration_image",),
    )
    inspiration_receipt = next(
        item for item in strict.workflows if item.category_key == "inspiration_image"
    )
    preview = _preview_items(inspiration_receipt.plan_hash)
    if not apply:
        return LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt(
            schema_version="legacy-workflow-release-receipt-v1",
            mode="preview",
            workflows=preview,
        )

    if inspiration_receipt.candidate_workflow_id is None:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_strict_source_missing", "严格灵感图候选未生成"
        )
    strict_workflow = db.get(
        WorkflowVersion, inspiration_receipt.candidate_workflow_id
    )
    if strict_workflow is None:
        raise LegacyWorkflowReleaseError(
            "legacy_release_strict_source_missing", "严格灵感图候选不存在"
        )
    base = _base_manifest(strict_workflow)
    by_key = {item.workflow_key: item for item in preview}

    _ensure_profile(db, actor=normalized_actor)
    legacy_prompt = _ensure_prompt(
        db,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        asset_key="legacy_doubao_single_prompt",
        stage="LEGACY",
        version=f"legacy-inspiration-{by_key['inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review'].source_fingerprint[:12]}",
        name="灵感图旧豆包单提示词",
        system_prompt=_prompt_text("inspiration_legacy_single_v1.txt"),
        actor=normalized_actor,
    )
    single_prompt = _ensure_prompt(
        db,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        asset_key="latest_single_prompt",
        stage="SINGLE",
        version=f"latest-inspiration-single-{by_key['inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison'].source_fingerprint[:12]}",
        name="灵感图最新机制单提示词",
        system_prompt=_prompt_text("inspiration_latest_single_v2.txt"),
        actor=normalized_actor,
    )
    case_image_prompt = _ensure_prompt(
        db,
        category_key="design_case",
        asset_key="case_image_review",
        stage="IMG",
        version=f"case-image-{by_key['design_case.legacy_doubao_review'].source_fingerprint[:12]}",
        name="案例逐图严格评测",
        system_prompt=_prompt_text("design_case_image_review_v1.txt"),
        actor=normalized_actor,
    )
    case_text_prompt = _ensure_prompt(
        db,
        category_key="design_case",
        asset_key="case_text_review",
        stage="CASE",
        version=f"case-text-{by_key['design_case.legacy_doubao_review'].source_fingerprint[:12]}",
        name="案例素材综合评审",
        system_prompt=_prompt_text("design_case_text_review_v1.txt"),
        actor=normalized_actor,
    )
    score_asset, score_rule = _ensure_case_score_rule(db, actor=normalized_actor)
    legacy_3d_su_prompts = {
        variant: _ensure_prompt(
            db,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            asset_key=spec["asset_key"],
            stage=spec["stage"],
            version=f"legacy-3d-su-{variant}-{by_key[spec['workflow_key']].source_fingerprint[:12]}",
            name=spec["prompt_name"],
            system_prompt=_prompt_text(spec["prompt_file"]),
            actor=normalized_actor,
        )
        for variant, spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.items()
    }

    manifests = {
        "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review": _legacy_inspiration_manifest(
            base,
            prompt=legacy_prompt,
            fingerprint=by_key["inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"].source_fingerprint,
        ),
        "inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison": _latest_ab_manifest(
            base,
            fingerprint=by_key["inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison"].source_fingerprint,
        ),
        "inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison": _latest_single_manifest(
            db,
            base,
            prompt=single_prompt,
            fingerprint=by_key["inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison"].source_fingerprint,
        ),
        "design_case.legacy_doubao_review": _case_manifest(
            base,
            image_prompt=case_image_prompt,
            text_prompt=case_text_prompt,
            score_asset=score_asset,
            score_rule=score_rule,
            fingerprint=by_key["design_case.legacy_doubao_review"].source_fingerprint,
        ),
    }
    for variant, spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.items():
        manifests[spec["workflow_key"]] = _legacy_3d_su_manifest(
            base,
            variant=variant,
            prompt=legacy_3d_su_prompts[variant],
            fingerprint=by_key[spec["workflow_key"]].source_fingerprint,
        )

    applied: list[LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem] = []
    for workflow_key, name, category_key in _RELEASE_SPECS:
        planned = by_key[workflow_key]
        definition, version = _ensure_workflow(
            db,
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            name=name,
            category_key=category_key,
            manifest=manifests[workflow_key],
            fingerprint=planned.source_fingerprint,
            actor=normalized_actor,
        )
        applied.append(
            LegacyWorkflowReceiptItem(
                workflow_key=workflow_key,
                category_key=category_key,
                version=version.version,
                source_fingerprint=planned.source_fingerprint,
                status=version.status,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                canonical_hash=version.canonical_hash,
            )
        )
    return LegacyWorkflowReleaseReceipt(
        schema_version="legacy-workflow-release-receipt-v1",
        mode="apply",
        workflows=tuple(applied),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/level_scale.py =====
"""Category-owned L-level scale with one global direction.

All categories share the machine meaning ``L1 = best`` and larger L numbers
mean lower quality.  A category may disable one or more of the canonical
``L1``..``L5`` buckets (for example use only L1-L4), rename their display
labels and move score cut-points.  It may not reverse the direction.

The module is pure and owns both backward-compatible ``level_thresholds``
parsing and the versioned ``level_scale`` contract.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any


DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION = "category-level-scale-v1"
CANONICAL_LEVELS: tuple[str, ...] = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
DEFAULT_THRESHOLDS: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...] = (
    {"min_score": 80, "level": "L1"},
    {"min_score": 60, "level": "L2"},
    {"min_score": 40, "level": "L3"},
    {"min_score": 20, "level": "L4"},
    {"min_score": 0, "level": "L5"},
)


class LevelScaleError(ValueError):
    """Stable fail-closed validation error for category level scales."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _is_int(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def _level_index(level: str) -> int:
    return CANONICAL_LEVELS.index(level)


def _normalize_thresholds(
    thresholds: Any, *, source: str
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(thresholds, (list, tuple)) or not thresholds:
        raise LevelScaleError("levels_invalid", "等级阈值必须是非空数组")

    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for entry in thresholds:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise LevelScaleError("level_invalid", "每个等级必须是对象")
        level = entry.get("level")
        min_score = entry.get("min_score")
        if level not in CANONICAL_LEVELS:
            raise LevelScaleError("level_unknown", "等级只能是 L1 至 L5")
        if level in seen:
            raise LevelScaleError("level_duplicate", f"等级重复：{level}")
        if not _is_int(min_score) or not 0 <= min_score <= 100:
            raise LevelScaleError(
                "min_score_invalid", f"{level}.min_score 必须是 0 至 100 的整数"
            )
        seen.add(level)
        normalized.append({"min_score": min_score, "level": level})

    normalized.sort(key=lambda item: _level_index(item["level"]))
    scores = [item["min_score"] for item in normalized]
    if any(left <= right for left, right in zip(scores, scores[1:])):
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "threshold_order_invalid", "等级阈值必须随 L 序号增大而严格下降"
        )
    if normalized[-1]["min_score"] != 0:
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "catch_all_missing", "最差的启用等级必须使用 min_score=0 兜底"
        )

    enabled = [item["level"] for item in normalized]
    return {
        "version": DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION,
        "source": source,
        "enabled_levels": enabled,
        "disabled_levels": [level for level in CANONICAL_LEVELS if level not in seen],
        "thresholds": normalized,
        "display_names": {level: level for level in enabled},
    }


def _normalize_level_scale(scale: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(scale, dict):
        raise LevelScaleError("scale_not_object", "level_scale 必须是对象")
    if scale.get("version") != DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION:
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "version_unsupported",
            f"level_scale.version 必须是 {DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION}",
        )
    levels = scale.get("levels")
    if not isinstance(levels, list) or not levels:
        raise LevelScaleError("levels_invalid", "level_scale.levels 必须是非空数组")

    seen: set[str] = set()
    enabled_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    display_names: dict[str, str] = {}
    disabled: list[str] = []
    for entry in levels:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise LevelScaleError("level_invalid", "level_scale.levels 每项必须是对象")
        level = entry.get("level")
        if level not in CANONICAL_LEVELS:
            raise LevelScaleError("level_unknown", "等级只能是 L1 至 L5")
        if level in seen:
            raise LevelScaleError("level_duplicate", f"等级重复：{level}")
        seen.add(level)

        enabled = entry.get("enabled")
        if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
            raise LevelScaleError("enabled_invalid", f"{level}.enabled 必须是布尔值")
        display_name = entry.get("display_name", level)
        if not isinstance(display_name, str) or not display_name.strip() or len(display_name) > 40:
            raise LevelScaleError(
                "display_name_invalid", f"{level}.display_name 必须是 1 至 40 字符"
            )

        if not enabled:
            if entry.get("min_score") is not None:
                raise LevelScaleError(
                    "disabled_level_threshold_present",
                    f"停用等级 {level} 不允许配置 min_score",
                )
            disabled.append(level)
            continue

        min_score = entry.get("min_score")
        if not _is_int(min_score) or not 0 <= min_score <= 100:
            raise LevelScaleError(
                "min_score_invalid", f"{level}.min_score 必须是 0 至 100 的整数"
            )
        enabled_entries.append({"min_score": min_score, "level": level})
        display_names[level] = display_name.strip()

    missing = [level for level in CANONICAL_LEVELS if level not in seen]
    if missing:
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "levels_incomplete", f"level_scale 必须显式声明 L1 至 L5；缺少：{','.join(missing)}"
        )
    if not enabled_entries:
        raise LevelScaleError("no_enabled_level", "至少启用一个等级")

    normalized = _normalize_thresholds(enabled_entries, source="level_scale")
    normalized["disabled_levels"] = [
        level for level in CANONICAL_LEVELS if level not in normalized["enabled_levels"]
    ]
    normalized["display_names"] = display_names
    return normalized


def resolve_level_scale(contract: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a normalized scale from a frozen category contract."""
    has_scale = contract.get("level_scale") is not None
    has_legacy = contract.get("level_thresholds") is not None
    if has_scale and has_legacy:
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "ambiguous_configuration",
            "level_scale 与旧 level_thresholds 不能同时配置",
        )
    if has_scale:
        return _normalize_level_scale(contract["level_scale"])
    if has_legacy:
        resolved = _normalize_thresholds(
            contract["level_thresholds"], source="legacy_level_thresholds"
        )
        # Historical threshold lists describe score buckets only.  Some active
        # contracts intentionally omit L5 from score mapping while reserving
        # it for redline output, so omission cannot be reinterpreted as an
        # explicit disabled level.  Only versioned ``level_scale`` can close a
        # level.
        resolved["enabled_levels"] = list(CANONICAL_LEVELS)
        resolved["disabled_levels"] = []
        resolved["display_names"] = {level: level for level in CANONICAL_LEVELS}
        return resolved
    return _normalize_thresholds(DEFAULT_THRESHOLDS, source="default")


def score_to_level(score: Any, resolved_scale: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Map an integer score to an enabled level."""
    if not _is_int(score):
        raise LevelScaleError("score_invalid", "分数必须是整数")
    for entry in resolved_scale["thresholds"]:
        if score >= entry["min_score"]:
            return entry["level"]
    return resolved_scale["thresholds"][-1]["level"]


def is_level_enabled(level: Any, resolved_scale: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return isinstance(level, str) and level in resolved_scale["enabled_levels"]


def assert_level_enabled(level: Any, resolved_scale: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Require a level to be enabled instead of silently remapping it."""
    if not is_level_enabled(level, resolved_scale):
        enabled = "、".join(resolved_scale.get("enabled_levels", ()))
        raise LevelScaleError(
            "level_not_enabled",
            f"等级 {level!r} 未在此类目启用；当前启用档为 {enabled}",
        )
    return level


def to_threshold_table(resolved_scale: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Project a normalized scale to the aggregator's legacy threshold shape."""
    thresholds = resolved_scale.get("thresholds")
    if not isinstance(thresholds, list) or not thresholds:
        raise LevelScaleError("scale_invalid", "等级表为空")
    return [
        {"min_score": entry["min_score"], "level": entry["level"]}
        for entry in thresholds
    ]

===== FILE backend/app/level_semantics.py =====
"""Single production meaning for all L-levels.

``L1`` is always best and larger numeric suffixes always mean lower quality.
Categories may disable buckets through their frozen level scale, but cannot
reverse this direction.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from .category_evaluation_aggregator import (
    LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION as UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION,
)


def describe_level_semantics(version: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Describe the sole known direction; unknown versions fail closed."""
    if version == UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION:
        return {
            "version": version,
            "known": True,
            "best_level": "L1",
            "worst_level": "L5",
            "direction": "L1=最优，L序号越大质量越差",
            "levels": {
                "L1": "best",
                "L2": "mid",
                "L3": "mid",
                "L4": "mid",
                "L5": "worst",
            },
        }
    return {
        "version": version,
        "known": False,
        "best_level": None,
        "worst_level": None,
        "direction": "unknown",
        "levels": {},
    }
