def _require_mysql(engine: Engine) -> None:
    if engine.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise ValueError("本地连接器端到端验收必须使用一次性 MySQL 8.4 schema")
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        database_name = connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT DATABASE()").scalar_one()
    if not isinstance(database_name, str) or re.fullmatch(
        r"labelsystem_test_(?:connector_)?[0-9a-f]{32}",
        database_name,
    ) is None:
        raise ValueError("本地连接器端到端验收必须使用一次性 MySQL schema")


def _create_fixture_tables(engine: Engine) -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE fixture_upstream_events ("
            "source_event_id VARCHAR(200) PRIMARY KEY, "
            "source_cursor BIGINT NOT NULL UNIQUE, "
            "category_key VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL, "
            "mechanism_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL DEFAULT '', "
            "payload_json LONGTEXT NOT NULL, "
            "source_occurred_at DATETIME(6) NULL)"
        )
        for table_name in _TARGET_TABLES:
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote(table_name)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"CREATE TABLE {quote} ("
                "business_idempotency_key VARCHAR(240) PRIMARY KEY, "
                "mechanism_version_id BIGINT NOT NULL, "
                "payload_hash CHAR(64) NOT NULL, "
                "payload_json LONGTEXT NOT NULL, "
                "external_record_key VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL, "
                "revision BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, "
                "review_state VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', "
                "published_at DATETIME(6) NULL)"
            )


def _schema_fingerprint(engine: Engine, table_name: str) -> str:
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        columns = [
            {"name": column["name"], "type": str(column["type"]).upper()}
            for column in sa_inspect(connection).get_columns(table_name)
        ]
    payload = json.dumps(
        {
            "table": table_name,
            "columns": sorted(columns, key=lambda item: item["name"]),
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _seed_upstream_rows(engine: Engine) -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        for scenario in _SCENARIOS:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "INSERT INTO fixture_upstream_events ("
                    "source_event_id, source_cursor, category_key, "
                    "mechanism_key, payload_json, source_occurred_at) VALUES ("
                    ":source_event_id, :source_cursor, :category_key, '', "
                    ":payload_json, :source_occurred_at)"
                ),
                {
                    "source_event_id": scenario.source_event_id,
                    "source_cursor": scenario.source_cursor,
                    "category_key": scenario.category_key,
                    "payload_json": canonical_json(scenario.payload),
                    "source_occurred_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                },
            )


class _SqlFixturePollAdapter(ConnectorPollAdapter):
    def __init__(
        self,
        engine: Engine,
        *,
        category_key: str,
        schema_fingerprint: str,
    ) -> None:
        self._engine = engine
        self._category_key = category_key
        self._schema_fingerprint = schema_fingerprint

    def verify_read_only(self) -> PollSafetyEvidence:
        return PollSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=True,
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
            verifier="disposable-mysql-read-only-query",
        )

    def fetch_page(self, *, after_cursor: str, limit: int) -> PollPage:
        watermark = int(after_cursor) if after_cursor else -1
        with self._engine.connect() as connection:
            rows = connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT source_event_id, source_cursor, "
                    "source_occurred_at, payload_json "
                    "FROM fixture_upstream_events "
                    "WHERE category_key = :category_key "
                    "AND source_cursor > :watermark "
                    "ORDER BY source_cursor ASC LIMIT :limit"
                ),
                {
                    "category_key": self._category_key,
                    "watermark": watermark,
                    "limit": limit,
                },
            ).mappings().all()
        records: list[PollRecord] = []
        for row in rows:
            payload = json.loads(str(row["payload_json"]))
            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                raise ValueError("上游夹具载荷必须是对象")
            records.append(
                PollRecord(
                    source_event_id=str(row["source_event_id"]),
                    source_cursor=str(row["source_cursor"]),
                    source_occurred_at=row["source_occurred_at"],
                    payload=payload,
                )
            )
        return PollPage(
            records=tuple(records),
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )


def _connector_node(
    *,
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": "读取上游数据" if kind == "upstream_connector" else "写入下游",
        "config": {
            "connector_key": contract.contract_key,
            "connector_contract_version_id": contract.id,
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["connector_contract_id", "payload_hash"],
    }


def _workflow_for_scenario(
    db: Session,
    scenario: LocalConnectorScenario,
    *,
    upstream: ConnectorContractVersion,
    downstream: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "upstream_connector")
    ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "downstream_projection")
    definition = create_workflow_definition(
        db,
        workflow_key=f"local.connector.{scenario.scenario_key}",
        name=f"本地连接器验收 · {scenario.scenario_key}",
        description="一次性 MySQL 影子环境连接器验收",
        owner="local-acceptance",
        allowed_categories=[scenario.category_key],
        created_by="local-acceptance",
    )
    nodes = [
        _connector_node(
            node_id="upstream",
            kind="upstream_connector",
            contract=upstream,
        ),
        _connector_node(
            node_id="downstream",
            kind="downstream_projection",
            contract=downstream,
        ),
    ]
    version = create_workflow_version(
        db,
        definition=definition,
        version="incremental-v1",
        manifest={
            "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
            "category_key": scenario.category_key,
            "lane_key": f"local_{scenario.scenario_key}",
            "run_modes": ["incremental"],
            "nodes": nodes,
            "edges": [{"from": "upstream", "to": "downstream"}],
            "queue_class": "production_batch",
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
            "layout": {
                "upstream": {"x": 60, "y": 120},
                "downstream": {"x": 420, "y": 120},
            },
        },
        created_by="local-acceptance",
    )
    version.status = "active"
    version.validation_report_json = canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []})
    db.flush()
    return version


def _register_contracts(
    db: Session,
    engine: Engine,
) -> tuple[
    dict[str, ConnectorContractVersion],
    dict[str, ConnectorContractVersion],
]:
    upstream_fingerprint = _schema_fingerprint(engine, _UPSTREAM_TABLE)
    upstream: dict[str, ConnectorContractVersion] = {}
    downstream: dict[str, ConnectorContractVersion] = {}
    for scenario in _SCENARIOS:
        if scenario.upstream_contract_key not in upstream:
            upstream[scenario.upstream_contract_key] = (
                create_connector_contract_version(
                    db,
                    contract_key=scenario.upstream_contract_key,
                    direction="upstream",
                    category_key=scenario.category_key,
                    logical_locator=(
                        f"logical.local_fixture.{scenario.category_key}.input"
                    ),
                    adapter_key="local-sql-read-v1",
                    schema_fingerprint=upstream_fingerprint,
                    field_mapping={"payload": "workflow_input"},
                    cursor_contract={
                        "field": "source_cursor",
                        "kind": "integer",
                        "ordering": "ascending",
                        "out_of_order": "delay",
                    },
                    idempotency_contract={"field": "source_event_id"},
                    batch_size=100,
                    timeout_seconds=30,
                    owner="local-acceptance",
                    status="active",
                    created_by="local-acceptance",
                )
            )
        downstream[scenario.downstream_contract_key] = (
            create_connector_contract_version(
                db,
                contract_key=scenario.downstream_contract_key,
                direction="downstream",
                category_key=scenario.category_key,
                logical_locator=(
                    f"logical.local_fixture.{scenario.scenario_key}.output"
                ),
                adapter_key="local-sql-envelope-v1",
                schema_fingerprint=_schema_fingerprint(
                    engine,
                    scenario.target_table,
                ),
                field_mapping={"result": "payload_json"},
                cursor_contract={},
                idempotency_contract={
                    "field": "business_idempotency_key"
                },
                batch_size=100,
                timeout_seconds=30,
                owner="local-acceptance",
                status="active",
                created_by="local-acceptance",
            )
        )
    return upstream, downstream


def _ensure_fixture_categories(db: Session) -> None:
    display_names = {
        "knowledge_graph": "知识图谱打标",
        "inspiration_image": "灵感图",
        "model_3d_su": "3D & SU 模型",
    }
    for category_key, display_name in display_names.items():
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
            )
        )
        if existing is not None:
            continue
        db.add(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key=category_key,
                display_name=display_name,
                description="一次性本地连接器验收类目",
                status="active",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                pipeline_config_json="{}",
                created_by="local-acceptance",
            )
        )
    db.flush()


def _execute_all_runtime_steps(engine: Engine) -> None:
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        processed = 0
        while True:
            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "local-connector-runtime",
            )
            db.commit()
            if attempt_id is None:
                break
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            if attempt is None or not attempt.lease_token:
                raise RuntimeError("连接器验收步骤缺少有效租约")
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
            if run is None:
                raise RuntimeError("连接器验收运行不存在")
            snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == attempt.step_key
            )
            result = execute_native_node(
                NodeExecutionContext(
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                    raw_fixture_output={},
                    attempt_no=attempt.attempt_no,
                    started_at=attempt.started_at,
                    input_fingerprint=attempt.input_hash,
                )
            )
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                result,
            )
            db.commit()
            processed += 1
            if processed > len(_SCENARIOS) * 4:
                raise RuntimeError("连接器验收工作流未在预期步骤数内结束")


def _deliver_all_targets(
    engine: Engine,
    downstream: dict[str, ConnectorContractVersion],
) -> None:
    table_by_contract = {
        item.downstream_contract_key: item.target_table for item in _SCENARIOS
    }
    with Session(engine) as db:
        for contract_key, contract in downstream.items():
            adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
                connection_factory=engine.begin,
                table_name=table_by_contract[contract_key],
                evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
                    least_privileged=True,
                    schema_fingerprint=contract.schema_fingerprint,
                    verifier="disposable-mysql-shadow-target",
                ),
            )
            deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                contract,
                worker_id="local-connector-delivery",
                adapter=adapter,
            )
            db.commit()


def run_local_connector_e2e(engine: Engine) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run four connector-only incremental paths against three shadow tables."""

    _require_mysql(engine)
    _create_fixture_tables(engine)
    _seed_upstream_rows(engine)
    scenario_state: dict[str, dict[str, int | str]] = {}
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        _ensure_fixture_categories(db)
        upstream, downstream = _register_contracts(db, engine)
        workflows = {
            scenario.scenario_key: _workflow_for_scenario(
                db,
                scenario,
                upstream=upstream[scenario.upstream_contract_key],
                downstream=downstream[scenario.downstream_contract_key],
            )
            for scenario in _SCENARIOS
        }
        db.commit()

        for contract_key, contract in upstream.items():
            category_key = next(
                item.category_key
                for item in _SCENARIOS
                if item.upstream_contract_key == contract_key
            )
            poll_connector_contract(
                db,
                contract.id,
                actor="local-connector-intake",
                adapter=_SqlFixturePollAdapter(
                    engine,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    schema_fingerprint=contract.schema_fingerprint,
                ),
            )
        db.flush()
        events = {
            item.source_event_id: item
            for item in db.scalars(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)).all()
        }
        for scenario in _SCENARIOS:
            event = events[scenario.source_event_id]
            workflow = workflows[scenario.scenario_key]
            run = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
                db,
                event_id=event.id,
                workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                actor="local-connector-runtime",
            )
            scenario_state[scenario.scenario_key] = {
                "event_id": event.id,
                "run_id": run.id,
                "workflow_version_id": workflow.id,
                "intake_contract_version_id": event.contract_version_id,
                "downstream_contract_id": downstream[
                    scenario.downstream_contract_key
                ].id,
            }
        db.commit()

    _execute_all_runtime_steps(engine)
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        downstream_rows = {
            item.contract_key: item
            for item in db.scalars(
                select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(
                    ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "downstream"
                )
            ).all()
        }
    _deliver_all_targets(engine, downstream_rows)

    scenarios: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    with Session(engine) as db:
        for scenario in _SCENARIOS:
            state = scenario_state[scenario.scenario_key]
            event = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, int(state["event_id"]))
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, int(state["run_id"]))
            outbox = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(
                    RuntimeOutboxEvent.mechanism_version_id
                    == int(state["workflow_version_id"]),
                    RuntimeOutboxEvent.target_contract_version_id
                    == int(state["downstream_contract_id"]),
                )
            )
            if event is None or run is None or outbox is None:
                raise RuntimeError("连接器验收事实不完整")
            snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
            nodes = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
            frozen_upstream = next(
                item["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                    "connector_contract_id"
                ]
                for item in nodes
                if item["kind"] == "upstream_connector"
            )
            frozen_downstream = next(
                item["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                    "connector_contract_id"
                ]
                for item in nodes
                if item["kind"] == "downstream_projection"
            )
            with engine.connect() as connection:
                quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote(
                    scenario.target_table
                )
                delivered = connection.execute(
                    text(
                        "SELECT mechanism_version_id, payload_hash "
                        f"FROM {quote} WHERE business_idempotency_key = :key"
                    ),
                    {"key": outbox.business_idempotency_key},
                ).mappings().one()
            scenario_report = {
                "scenario_key": scenario.scenario_key,
                "category_key": scenario.category_key,
                "target_table": scenario.target_table,
                "intake_disposition": event.disposition,
                "intake_contract_version_id": event.contract_version_id,
                "run_status": run.status,
                "workflow_version_id": run.workflow_version_id,
                "outbox_status": outbox.status,
                "outbox_contract_version_id": (
                    outbox.target_contract_version_id
                ),
                "outbox_payload_hash": outbox.payload_hash,
                "frozen_upstream_contract_id": frozen_upstream,
                "frozen_downstream_contract_id": frozen_downstream,
                "delivered_mechanism_version_id": int(
                    delivered["mechanism_version_id"]
                ),
                "delivered_payload_hash": str(delivered["payload_hash"]),
            }
            scenario_report["passed"] = all(
                (
                    scenario_report["intake_disposition"] == "accepted",
                    scenario_report["run_status"] == "succeeded",
                    scenario_report["outbox_status"] == "delivered",
                    frozen_upstream == event.contract_version_id,
                    frozen_downstream == outbox.target_contract_version_id,
                    int(delivered["mechanism_version_id"])
                    == run.workflow_version_id,
                    str(delivered["payload_hash"]) == outbox.payload_hash,
                )
            )
            scenarios.append(scenario_report)

    target_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        for table_name in _TARGET_TABLES:
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote(table_name)
            target_counts[table_name] = int(
                connection.scalar(text(f"SELECT count(*) FROM {quote}")) or 0
            )
        server_version = str(connection.dialect.server_version_info)
    return {
        "schema_version": "local-connector-e2e-v1",
        "dialect": engine.dialect.name,
        "server_version": server_version,
        "external_model_calls": 0,
        "external_network_calls": 0,
        "shadow_writes": sum(target_counts.values()),
        "target_counts": target_counts,
        "scenarios": scenarios,
        "all_passed": all(item["passed"] for item in scenarios),
    }


def run_local_connector_fault_matrix(engine: Engine) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run the connector failure matrix in a disposable MySQL schema."""

    _require_mysql(engine)
    _create_fixture_tables(engine)
    label_fingerprint = _schema_fingerprint(engine, "shadow_labels")

    def upstream_contract(db: Session, suffix: str) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
        return create_connector_contract_version(
            db,
            contract_key=f"fault-{suffix}-input",
            direction="upstream",
            category_key="knowledge_graph",
            logical_locator=f"logical.local_fault.{suffix}.input",
            adapter_key="local-sql-read-v1",
            schema_fingerprint=_schema_fingerprint(engine, _UPSTREAM_TABLE),
            field_mapping={"payload": "workflow_input"},
            cursor_contract={
                "field": "source_cursor",
                "kind": "integer",
                "ordering": "ascending",
                "out_of_order": "delay",
            },
            idempotency_contract={"field": "source_event_id"},
            batch_size=100,
            timeout_seconds=30,
            owner="local-fault-matrix",
            status="active",
            created_by="local-fault-matrix",
        )

    def downstream_contract(
        db: Session,
        suffix: str,
    ) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
        return create_connector_contract_version(
            db,
            contract_key=f"fault-{suffix}-output",
            direction="downstream",
            category_key="knowledge_graph",
            logical_locator=f"logical.local_fault.{suffix}.output",
            adapter_key="local-sql-envelope-v1",
            schema_fingerprint=label_fingerprint,
            field_mapping={"result": "payload_json"},
            cursor_contract={},
            idempotency_contract={"field": "business_idempotency_key"},
            batch_size=100,
            timeout_seconds=30,
            owner="local-fault-matrix",
            status="active",
            created_by="local-fault-matrix",
        )

    def intake(
        db: Session,
        contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
        *,
        event_id: str,
        cursor: int,
        value: str,
    ):
        return ingest_upstream_event(
            db,
            contract,
            IntakeRequest(
                source_event_id=event_id,
                source_cursor=str(cursor),
                source_occurred_at=None,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                mechanism_key="",
                payload={
                    "content_key": event_id,
                    "sequence": cursor,
                    "value": value,
                },
                actor="local-fault-matrix",
            ),
        )

    def enqueue(
        db: Session,
        *,
        mechanism: WorkflowVersion,
        target: ConnectorContractVersion,
        suffix: str,
        max_attempts: int = 3,
    ) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
        return enqueue_runtime_outbox(
            db,
            RuntimeOutboxRequest(
                event_key=f"fault-delivery:{suffix}",
                target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
                target_contract_version_id=target.id,
                business_idempotency_key=f"fault:{suffix}",
                mechanism_version_id=mechanism.id,
                payload={
                    "schema_version": "local-fault-payload-v1",
                    "content_key": suffix,
                },
                max_attempts=max_attempts,
            ),
        )

    def fixture_adapter(
        *,
        least_privileged: bool = True,
        fingerprint: str | None = None,
        fail_once_keys: set[str] | None = None,
        corrupt_readback_keys: set[str] | None = None,
    ) -> FixtureDownstreamAdapter:
        return FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
            schema_fingerprint=fingerprint or label_fingerprint,
            least_privileged=least_privileged,
            fail_once_keys=fail_once_keys,
            corrupt_readback_keys=corrupt_readback_keys,
        )

    cases: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def record(key: str, passed: bool, evidence: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        cases.append({"key": key, "passed": bool(passed), "evidence": evidence})

    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        _ensure_fixture_categories(db)
        base_upstream = upstream_contract(db, "base")
        base_downstream = downstream_contract(db, "base")
        mechanism = _workflow_for_scenario(
            db,
            _SCENARIOS[0],
            upstream=base_upstream,
            downstream=base_downstream,
        )
        db.commit()

        duplicate = upstream_contract(db, "duplicate")
        first = intake(
            db,
            duplicate,
            event_id="fault-duplicate-1",
            cursor=10,
            value="same",
        )
        second = intake(
            db,
            duplicate,
            event_id="fault-duplicate-1",
            cursor=10,
            value="same",
        )
        db.commit()
        record(
            "duplicate_event",
            first.event.id == second.event.id and second.idempotent_replay,
            {
                "same_fact_id": first.event.id == second.event.id,
                "idempotent_replay": second.idempotent_replay,
            },
        )

        conflict_code = None
        try:
            intake(
                db,
                duplicate,
                event_id="fault-duplicate-1",
                cursor=10,
                value="changed",
            )
        except ConnectorIntakeError as exc:
            conflict_code = exc.code
            db.rollback()
        record(
            "same_id_payload_conflict",
            conflict_code == "intake_idempotency_conflict",
            {"error_code": conflict_code},
        )

        ordering = upstream_contract(db, "ordering")
        accepted = intake(
            db,
            ordering,
            event_id="fault-order-10",
            cursor=10,
            value="newer",
        )
        delayed = intake(
            db,
            ordering,
            event_id="fault-order-9",
            cursor=9,
            value="older",
        )
        db.commit()
        record(
            "out_of_order_cursor",
            accepted.disposition == "accepted"
            and delayed.disposition == "delayed"
            and delayed.out_of_order,
            {
                "first": accepted.disposition,
                "stale": delayed.disposition,
            },
        )

        drift_target = downstream_contract(db, "schema-drift")
        drift_event = enqueue(
            db,
            mechanism=mechanism,
            target=drift_target,
            suffix="schema-drift",
        )
        db.commit()
        drift_code = None
        try:
            deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                drift_target,
                worker_id="fault-schema-drift",
                adapter=fixture_adapter(fingerprint="0" * 64),
            )
        except ConnectorDeliveryError as exc:
            drift_code = exc.code
            db.rollback()
        stored_drift = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, drift_event.id)
        record(
            "schema_drift",
            drift_code == "downstream_schema_drift"
            and stored_drift is not None
            and stored_drift.status == "queued"
            and stored_drift.attempt_count == 0,
            {"error_code": drift_code, "claimed": False},
        )

        permission_target = downstream_contract(db, "permission")
        permission_event = enqueue(
            db,
            mechanism=mechanism,
            target=permission_target,
            suffix="permission",
        )
        db.commit()
        permission_code = None
        try:
            deliver_outbox_batch(
                db,
                permission_target,
                worker_id="fault-permission",
                adapter=fixture_adapter(least_privileged=False),
            )
        except ConnectorDeliveryError as exc:
            permission_code = exc.code
            db.rollback()
        stored_permission = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, permission_event.id)
        record(
            "permission_overreach",
            permission_code == "downstream_permission_overbroad"
            and stored_permission is not None
            and stored_permission.status == "queued"
            and stored_permission.attempt_count == 0,
            {"error_code": permission_code, "claimed": False},
        )

        connection_target = downstream_contract(db, "connection-loss")
        connection_event = enqueue(
            db,
            mechanism=mechanism,
            target=connection_target,
            suffix="connection-loss",
        )
        db.commit()

        class ConnectionLossAdapter(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
            def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch):
                raise ConnectionError("simulated unavailable target")

        connection_result = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            connection_target,
            worker_id="fault-connection",
            adapter=ConnectionLossAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=label_fingerprint,
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        stored_connection = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, connection_event.id)
        record(
            "connection_loss",
            connection_result.retry_event_ids == (connection_event.id,)
            and stored_connection is not None
            and stored_connection.status == "queued"
            and stored_connection.last_error_code
            == "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
            {
                "status": (
                    stored_connection.status if stored_connection else None
                ),
                "retryable": connection_event.id
                in connection_result.retry_event_ids,
            },
        )

        partial_target = downstream_contract(db, "partial")
        partial_events = [
            enqueue(
                db,
                mechanism=mechanism,
                target=partial_target,
                suffix=f"partial-{suffix}",
            )
            for suffix in ("a", "b", "c")
        ]
        db.commit()
        failed_key = "fault:partial-b"
        partial_adapter = fixture_adapter(fail_once_keys={failed_key})
        first_batch = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            partial_target,
            worker_id="fault-partial",
            adapter=partial_adapter,
        )
        db.commit()
        failed_event = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, partial_events[1].id)
        if failed_event is not None:
            failed_event.available_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        db.commit()
        second_batch = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            partial_target,
            worker_id="fault-partial-retry",
            adapter=partial_adapter,
        )
        db.commit()
        record(
            "partial_batch",
            first_batch.retry_event_ids == (partial_events[1].id,)
            and second_batch.delivered_event_ids == (partial_events[1].id,)
            and partial_adapter.attempted_batches[-1] == (failed_key,),
            {
                "first_delivered": len(first_batch.delivered_event_ids),
                "retry_count": len(second_batch.delivered_event_ids),
            },
        )

        sql_adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
            connection_factory=engine.begin,
            table_name="shadow_labels",
            evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
                least_privileged=True,
                schema_fingerprint=label_fingerprint,
                verifier="disposable-mysql-shadow-target",
            ),
        )
        unique_key = "fault:unique-conflict"
        initial_key = DeliveryKey(unique_key, mechanism.id, "a" * 64)
        conflict_key = DeliveryKey(unique_key, mechanism.id + 1, "b" * 64)
        initial_delivery = sql_adapter.deliver(
            DeliveryBatch(
                target_contract_key="fault-unique-output",
                contract_version=1,
                items=(
                    DeliveryItem(
                        event_id=1,
                        key=initial_key,
                        payload={"value": "original"},
                    ),
                ),
            )
        )
        conflicting_delivery = sql_adapter.deliver(
            DeliveryBatch(
                target_contract_key="fault-unique-output",
                contract_version=1,
                items=(
                    DeliveryItem(
                        event_id=2,
                        key=conflict_key,
                        payload={"value": "changed"},
                    ),
                ),
            )
        )
        stored_unique = sql_adapter.read_back((initial_key,))
        record(
            "unique_conflict",
            initial_delivery.results[0].succeeded
            and not conflicting_delivery.results[0].succeeded
            and conflicting_delivery.results[0].error_code
            == "downstream_identity_conflict"
            and len(stored_unique) == 1
            and stored_unique[0].key == initial_key,
            {
                "conflict_code": conflicting_delivery.results[0].error_code,
                "stored_rows": len(stored_unique),
            },
        )

        mismatch_target = downstream_contract(db, "readback")
        mismatch_event = enqueue(
            db,
            mechanism=mechanism,
            target=mismatch_target,
            suffix="readback",
            max_attempts=1,
        )
        db.commit()
        mismatch_result = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            mismatch_target,
            worker_id="fault-readback",
            adapter=fixture_adapter(
                corrupt_readback_keys={"fault:readback"},
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        stored_mismatch = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, mismatch_event.id)
        record(
            "read_back_mismatch",
            mismatch_result.blocked_event_ids == (mismatch_event.id,)
            and stored_mismatch is not None
            and stored_mismatch.status == "dead_lettered"
            and stored_mismatch.last_error_code
            == "downstream_readback_mismatch",
            {
                "status": stored_mismatch.status if stored_mismatch else None,
                "error_code": (
                    stored_mismatch.last_error_code
                    if stored_mismatch
                    else None
                ),
            },
        )

        pause_contract = upstream_contract(db, "pause")
        pause_state = set_connector_pause_state(
            db,
            category_key="knowledge_graph",
            mechanism_key="",
            contract_key=pause_contract.contract_key,
            state="paused",
            reason="故障矩阵验证暂停接收",
            expected_revision=None,
            actor="local-fault-matrix",
        )
        db.commit()
        pause_code = None
        try:
            intake(
                db,
                pause_contract,
                event_id="fault-pause-1",
                cursor=1,
                value="paused",
            )
        except ConnectorIntakeError as exc:
            pause_code = exc.code
            db.rollback()
        resumed = set_connector_pause_state(
            db,
            category_key="knowledge_graph",
            mechanism_key="",
            contract_key=pause_contract.contract_key,
            state="active",
            reason="故障矩阵验证恢复接收",
            expected_revision=pause_state.revision,
            actor="local-fault-matrix",
        )
        resumed_event = intake(
            db,
            pause_contract,
            event_id="fault-pause-1",
            cursor=1,
            value="resumed",
        )
        db.commit()
        record(
            "pause_resume",
            pause_code == "upstream_connector_paused"
            and resumed.state == "active"
            and resumed_event.disposition == "accepted",
            {
                "pause_error_code": pause_code,
                "resumed_revision": resumed.revision,
            },
        )

        compensation_target = downstream_contract(db, "compensation")
        compensation_event = enqueue(
            db,
            mechanism=mechanism,
            target=compensation_target,
            suffix="compensation",
            max_attempts=1,
        )
        db.commit()
        bad_compensation = fixture_adapter(
            corrupt_readback_keys={"fault:compensation"},
        )
        deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            compensation_target,
            worker_id="fault-compensation-initial",
            adapter=bad_compensation,
        )
        db.commit()
        replay_dead_letter_delivery(
            db,
            compensation_event.id,
            actor="local-fault-matrix",
            reason="验证补偿失败仍保留死信",
        )
        db.commit()
        failed_compensation = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            compensation_target,
            worker_id="fault-compensation-retry",
            adapter=bad_compensation,
        )
        db.commit()
        stored_compensation = db.get(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent,
            compensation_event.id,
        )
        record(
            "compensation_failure",
            failed_compensation.blocked_event_ids
            == (compensation_event.id,)
            and stored_compensation is not None
            and stored_compensation.status == "dead_lettered"
            and stored_compensation.attempt_count == 2,
            {
                "status": (
                    stored_compensation.status
                    if stored_compensation
                    else None
                ),
                "attempt_count": (
                    stored_compensation.attempt_count
                    if stored_compensation
                    else None
                ),
            },
        )

        replay_dead_letter_delivery(
            db,
            compensation_event.id,
            actor="local-fault-matrix",
            reason="验证修复后死信可以安全重放",
        )
        db.commit()
        recovered = deliver_outbox_batch(
            db,
            compensation_target,
            worker_id="fault-dead-letter-replay",
            adapter=fixture_adapter(),
        )
        db.commit()
        recovered_event = db.get(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent,
            compensation_event.id,
        )
        record(
            "dead_letter_replay",
            recovered.delivered_event_ids == (compensation_event.id,)
            and recovered_event is not None
            and recovered_event.status == "delivered",
            {
                "status": recovered_event.status if recovered_event else None,
                "delivered": compensation_event.id
                in recovered.delivered_event_ids,
            },
        )

        restart_key = DeliveryKey(
            "fault:application-restart",
            mechanism.id,
            "c" * 64,
        )
        restart_batch = DeliveryBatch(
            target_contract_key="fault-restart-output",
            contract_version=1,
            items=(
                DeliveryItem(
                    event_id=3,
                    key=restart_key,
                    payload={"value": "persisted"},
                ),
            ),
        )
        before_restart = sql_adapter.deliver(restart_batch)
        restarted_adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
            connection_factory=engine.begin,
            table_name="shadow_labels",
            evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
                least_privileged=True,
                schema_fingerprint=label_fingerprint,
                verifier="new-adapter-after-restart",
            ),
        )
        after_restart = restarted_adapter.deliver(restart_batch)
        restarted_rows = restarted_adapter.read_back((restart_key,))
        record(
            "application_restart",
            before_restart.results[0].succeeded
            and after_restart.results[0].succeeded
            and len(restarted_rows) == 1
            and restarted_rows[0].key == restart_key,
            {
                "idempotent_after_restart": (
                    after_restart.results[0].succeeded
                ),
                "read_back_rows": len(restarted_rows),
            },
        )

    with engine.connect() as connection:
        server_version = str(connection.dialect.server_version_info)
    return {
        "schema_version": "local-connector-fault-matrix-v1",
        "dialect": engine.dialect.name,
        "server_version": server_version,
        "external_model_calls": 0,
        "external_network_calls": 0,
        "cases": cases,
        "all_passed": (
            {item["key"] for item in cases}
            == set(LOCAL_CONNECTOR_FAULT_CASES)
            and all(item["passed"] for item in cases)
        ),
    }

===== FILE backend/app/local_hardware_cost.py =====
"""Whole-machine scenario estimates; never rewrite provider billing facts."""
from decimal import Decimal
import json
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator
from sqlalchemy import select
from .models import LocalHardwareCostProfile


class HardwareAssumptions(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", allow_inf_nan=False)
    purchase_yuan: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=100_000_000)
    residual_yuan: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=100_000_000)
    depreciation_months: int = Field(ge=1, le=240, strict=True)
    allocated_hours_per_month: Decimal = Field(ge=1, le=744)
    average_power_kw: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=1000)
    electricity_yuan_per_kwh: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=1000)
    operations_yuan_per_month: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=10_000_000)
    successful_materials_per_hour: Decimal = Field(ge=Decimal("0.001"), le=10_000_000)
    evidence_reference: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1000)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_assumptions(self):
        if self.residual_yuan > self.purchase_yuan:
            raise ValueError("残值不能超过设备成本")
        if not self.evidence_reference.strip():
            raise ValueError("须填写参数及吞吐量的依据")
        return self


def estimate_hardware_cost(assumptions: HardwareAssumptions, count: int = 1):
    if isinstance(count, bool) or not isinstance(count, int) or not 1 <= count <= 10_000_000:
        raise ValueError("素材数量须为 1–10000000 的整数")
    a = assumptions
    depreciation = (a.purchase_yuan-a.residual_yuan) / a.depreciation_months / a.allocated_hours_per_month
    electricity = a.average_power_kw*a.electricity_yuan_per_kwh
    operations = a.operations_yuan_per_month/a.allocated_hours_per_month
    hourly = depreciation+electricity+operations
    hours = Decimal(count)/a.successful_materials_per_hour
    def number(value): return float(value.quantize(Decimal("0.00000001")))
    return {"formula_version":"whole-machine-v1", "kind":"scenario_estimate", "currency":"CNY",
        "material_count":count, "estimated_hours":number(hours), "hourly_yuan":number(hourly),
        "per_material_yuan":number(hourly/a.successful_materials_per_hour), "total_yuan":number(hourly*hours),
        "hourly_components":{"depreciation":number(depreciation), "electricity":number(electricity), "operations":number(operations)},
        "billing_ledger_changed":False,
        "notes":["按整机有效吞吐量分摊，不能按每个并发请求耗时重复累计整机费用。",
                 "吞吐量须来自同一模型、图片规格、工作流和并发配置，包含失败及重试消耗。",
                 "分摊时数包含本服务承担的空闲成本；与其他服务共用机器时，应先确定成本归属。",
                 "这是参数情景估算，不是实测电费或供应商账单，不替代历史调用账本。"]}


def current_profile(db, machine_key):
    return db.scalar(select(LocalHardwareCostProfile).where(LocalHardwareCostProfile.machine_key==machine_key)
        .order_by(LocalHardwareCostProfile.revision.desc()).limit(1))


def profile_payload(row):
    if row is None: return {"configured":False,"revision":0,"assumptions":None,"estimate":None}
    assumptions=HardwareAssumptions.model_validate_json(row.assumptions_json)
    return {"configured":True,"revision":row.revision,"assumptions":json.loads(assumptions.model_dump_json()),
        "estimate":estimate_hardware_cost(assumptions),"actor":row.actor,"created_at":row.created_at}

===== FILE backend/app/local_hardware_cost_api.py =====
import json
from typing import Literal
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .database import get_db
from .authz import has_permission
from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .models import LocalHardwareCostProfile
from .local_hardware_cost import HardwareAssumptions, current_profile, profile_payload, estimate_hardware_cost


class HardwareEstimateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config=ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    assumptions: HardwareAssumptions
    material_count: int = Field(default=1,ge=1,le=10_000_000,strict=True)


class HardwareProfileRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config=ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0,strict=True)
    assumptions: HardwareAssumptions


def build_local_hardware_cost_router(require_user, require_admin):
    router=APIRouter(prefix="/api/local-hardware-cost",tags=["local-hardware-cost"])
    def require_reader(user=Depends(require_user)):
        if not has_permission(user,"models:read"):
            raise HTTPException(403,"无权查看模型成本")
        return user

    @router.get("/{machine_key}")
    def read(machine_key: Literal["qwen205"], user=Depends(require_reader), db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        return {**profile_payload(current_profile(db,machine_key)),"machine_key":machine_key,"can_edit":bool(user.is_admin)}

    @router.post("/estimate")
    def estimate(payload:HardwareEstimateRequest, user=Depends(require_reader)):
        return estimate_hardware_cost(payload.assumptions,payload.material_count)

    @router.post("/{machine_key}")
    def save(machine_key:Literal["qwen205"],payload:HardwareProfileRequest,user=Depends(require_admin),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        old=current_profile(db,machine_key)
        if (old.revision if old else 0)!=payload.expected_revision:
            raise HTTPException(409,"成本参数已变化，请刷新后重试")
        row=LocalHardwareCostProfile(machine_key=machine_key,revision=payload.expected_revision+1,
            assumptions_json=payload.assumptions.model_dump_json(),actor=user.username)
        db.add(row)
        try:
            db.flush()
            append_audit_event(db,category="model_cost",action="hardware_assumptions_versioned",subject_type="local_hardware_cost_profile",
                subject_id=row.id,actor=user.username,event_key=f"hardware-cost:{machine_key}:{row.revision}",
                payload={"machine_key":machine_key,"revision":row.revision,"assumptions":json.loads(row.assumptions_json),"billing_ledger_changed":False})
            db.commit()
        except IntegrityError:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409,"成本参数已被其他管理员更新，请刷新后重试") from None
        return profile_payload(row)
    return router

===== FILE backend/app/login_throttle.py =====
"""Database-backed login admission shared by API processes.

Reserve before password hashing, commit before verification, never keep a DB
lock across scrypt. Successful authentication resets only its account window.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import math
from sqlalchemy import delete, select, update
from sqlalchemy.dialects.mysql import insert as mysql_insert
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.dialects.sqlite import insert as sqlite_insert
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .models import LoginAttemptWindow


class LoginThrottled(Exception):
    def __init__(self, retry_after: int):
        self.retry_after = retry_after


def reserve_login_attempt(db: Session, *, scope: str, identity: str, limit: int,
                          window_seconds: int, now: datetime | None = None) -> str:
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    key = hashlib.sha256((scope + ":" + identity).encode()).hexdigest()
    expires = now + timedelta(seconds=window_seconds)
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    values = dict(key_hash=key, attempts=0, expires_at=expires)
    if dialect == "mysql":
        statement = mysql_insert(LoginAttemptWindow).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_duplicate_key_update(key_hash=statement.inserted.key_hash)
    elif dialect in {"sqlite", "postgresql"}:
        factory = sqlite_insert if dialect == "sqlite" else pg_insert
        statement = factory(LoginAttemptWindow).values(**values).on_conflict_do_nothing(index_elements=["key_hash"])
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("Unsupported login throttle database")
    db.execute(statement)
    db.execute(update(LoginAttemptWindow).where(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash == key,
        LoginAttemptWindow.expires_at <= now).values(attempts=0, expires_at=expires))
    reserved = db.execute(update(LoginAttemptWindow).where(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash == key,
        LoginAttemptWindow.attempts < limit).values(attempts=LoginAttemptWindow.attempts + 1)).rowcount
    until = db.scalar(select(LoginAttemptWindow.expires_at).where(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash == key))
    if scope == "peer":
        prune_expired_windows(db)
    db.commit()
    if not reserved:
        assert until is not None
        if until.tzinfo is None: until = until.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        raise LoginThrottled(max(1, math.ceil((until-now).total_seconds())))
    return key


def clear_account_window(db: Session, key: str) -> None:
    db.execute(delete(LoginAttemptWindow).where(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash == key))


def prune_expired_windows(db: Session) -> None:
    # Bounded cleanup on peer admission; indexed expiry avoids an unbounded
    # request-time table scan. No need to retain attack identifiers indefinitely.
    cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=1)
    keys = list(db.scalars(select(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash).where(
        LoginAttemptWindow.expires_at < cutoff).limit(100)))
    if keys:
        db.execute(delete(LoginAttemptWindow).where(LoginAttemptWindow.key_hash.in_(keys)))

class LoopContractError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a loop command violates a frozen workflow contract."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LoopDecision:
    status: str
    machine_converged: bool
    needs_human: bool
    reason_codes: tuple[str, ...]
    evidence: dict[str, Any]
    next_round: int | None = None
    next_kind: str | None = None
    target_dimensions: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "status": self.status,
            "machine_converged": self.machine_converged,
            "needs_human": self.needs_human,
            "reason_codes": list(self.reason_codes),
            "evidence": self.evidence,
            "next_round": self.next_round,
            "next_kind": self.next_kind,
            "target_dimensions": list(self.target_dimensions),
        }


def canonical_json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def request_fingerprint(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _normalized_key(value: object) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", str(value).lower())


def _key_tokens(value: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    separated = re.sub(r"([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", r"\1_\2", str(value))
    return tuple(
        token
        for token in re.split(r"[^a-z0-9]+", separated.lower())
        if token
    )


def _is_sensitive_key(value: object) -> bool:
    normalized = _normalized_key(value)
    tokens = set(_key_tokens(value))
    if normalized == "key":
        return False
    if (
        normalized in _SENSITIVE_NORMALIZED_KEYS
        or normalized.endswith(_SENSITIVE_KEY_SUFFIXES)
    ):
        return True
    if any(
        root in normalized
        for root in (
            "apikey",
            "authorization",
            "authtoken",
            "clientsecret",
            "providerpayload",
            "proxyauthorization",
            "sessiontoken",
            "xauthtoken",
        )
    ) and any(
        container in normalized
        for container in (
            "backup",
            "container",
            "data",
            "header",
            "payload",
            "snapshot",
            "value",
        )
    ):
        return True
    if tokens & {
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "credential",
        "credentials",
        "password",
        "passwd",
    }:
        return True
    if "secret" in tokens and tokens & {
        "api",
        "auth",
        "client",
        "credential",
        "key",
        "token",
        "value",
        "backup",
        "container",
        "header",
        "payload",
    }:
        return True
    if "token" in tokens and tokens & {
        "access",
        "auth",
        "bearer",
        "client",
        "header",
        "id",
        "refresh",
        "session",
        "value",
        "x",
        "backup",
        "container",
        "payload",
    }:
        return True
    if "key" in tokens and tokens & {
        "access",
        "api",
        "auth",
        "private",
        "secret",
        "signing",
        "x",
    }:
        return True
    if "header" in tokens and tokens & {
        "api",
        "auth",
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "token",
    }:
        return True
    return bool(
        "payload" in tokens
        and tokens
        & {
            "auth",
            "backup",
            "credential",
            "provider",
            "raw",
            "request",
            "response",
            "secret",
            "token",
        }
    )


def _is_credential_auth_token(scheme: str, token: str) -> bool:
    """Recognize credential-shaped auth values without flagging prose."""
    normalized_scheme = scheme.lower()
    lowered_token = token.lower()
    if lowered_token in {
        "auth",
        "authentication",
        "authorization",
        "mechanism",
        "mode",
        "scheme",
        "token",
    }:
        return False

    if normalized_scheme == "basic":
        try:
            padded = token + ("=" * (-len(token) % 4))
            decoded = base64.b64decode(
                padded.encode("ascii"),
                altchars=b"-_",
                validate=True,
            )
        except (UnicodeEncodeError, binascii.Error, ValueError):
            decoded = b""
        if b":" in decoded:
            return True

    if re.fullmatch(r"(?i)sk-[a-z0-9_-]{3,}", token):
        return True
    if re.fullmatch(
        r"(?i)eyj[a-z0-9_-]{3,}\.[a-z0-9_-]{3,}\.[a-z0-9_-]{3,}",
        token,
    ):
        return True

    minimum_length = 8 if normalized_scheme == "basic" else 12
    if len(token) < minimum_length:
        return False
    if normalized_scheme == "bearer":
        return True
    has_digit = any(character.isdigit() for character in token)
    has_mixed_case = (
        any(character.islower() for character in token)
        and any(character.isupper() for character in token)
    )
    has_token_punctuation = any(
        character in "._~+/=" for character in token
    )
    has_multiple_hyphens = token.count("-") >= 2
    return (
        has_digit
        or has_mixed_case
        or has_token_punctuation
        or has_multiple_hyphens
    )


def contains_credential_auth_scheme(value: str) -> bool:
    return any(
        _is_credential_auth_token(
            match.group("scheme"),
            match.group("token"),
        )
        for match in _AUTH_SCHEME_CANDIDATE_RE.finditer(value)
    )


def _string_contains_auth_material(value: str) -> bool:
    if (
        _STRING_SECRET_ALIAS_VALUE_RE.search(value)
        or _AUTH_HEADER_VALUE_RE.search(value)
        or contains_credential_auth_scheme(value)
    ):
        return True
    for match in _URL_RE.finditer(value):
        candidate = match.group(0).rstrip(".,);]")
        try:
            parts = urlsplit(candidate)
        except ValueError:
            return True
        if parts.username is not None or parts.password is not None:
            return True
        for key, item_value in parse_qsl(parts.query, keep_blank_values=True):
            if _normalized_key(key) in _URL_SECRET_QUERY_KEYS and item_value:
                return True
    return False


def assert_safe_normalized_payload(value: Any, path: str = "$") -> None:
    """Reject dependency payloads and credential-shaped fields at the API boundary."""
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for raw_key, item in value.items():
            tokens = set(_key_tokens(raw_key))
            if (
                _is_sensitive_key(raw_key)
                or (
                    isinstance(raw_key, str)
                    and _string_contains_auth_material(raw_key)
                )
                or (
                    "raw" in tokens
                    and tokens & {"payload", "request", "response"}
                )
            ):
                raise LoopContractError(
                    f"{path}.* 不允许包含原始或敏感字段"
                )
            assert_safe_normalized_payload(item, f"{path}.*")
    elif isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        for index, item in enumerate(value):
            assert_safe_normalized_payload(item, f"{path}[{index}]")
    elif isinstance(value, str) and _string_contains_auth_material(value):
        raise LoopContractError(f"{path} 不允许包含认证材料")


def _dimension_values(value: Any) -> Iterable[str]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        yield from (str(key).strip() for key in value if str(key).strip())
    elif isinstance(value, Sequence) and not isinstance(value, (str, bytes)):
        for item in value:
            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip():
                yield item.strip()
            elif isinstance(item, Mapping):
                dimension = item.get("dimension") or item.get("field")
                if isinstance(dimension, str) and dimension.strip():
                    yield dimension.strip()


def problem_dimensions(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    dimensions: set[str] = set()
    for field in PROBLEM_DIMENSION_FIELDS:
        dimensions.update(_dimension_values(result.get(field)))
    dimensions.update(_dimension_values(result.get("conflicts")))
    for field, sentinel in RULE_SENTINELS.items():
        if result.get(field) is False:
            dimensions.add(sentinel)
    return tuple(sorted(dimensions))


def conflict_dimensions(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    dimensions = set(_dimension_values(result.get("ab_conflict_dimensions")))
    dimensions.update(_dimension_values(result.get("conflicts")))
    return tuple(sorted(dimensions))


def _attempt_result(attempt: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    result = attempt.get("normalized_result")
    if not isinstance(result, Mapping):
        raise LoopContractError("完成轮次必须包含 normalized_result 对象")
    return result


def _round(attempt: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    value = attempt.get("round", attempt.get("business_round"))
    if not isinstance(value, int) or not 1 <= value <= MAX_BUSINESS_ROUNDS:
        raise LoopContractError("业务轮次只能是 1 至 3")
    return value


def _nonempty_mapping(value: Any) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    return value if isinstance(value, Mapping) and bool(value) else None


def _dimension_result_values(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    values: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for field in ("dimension_values", "suggested_values"):
        field_value = result.get(field)
        if isinstance(field_value, Mapping):
            values.update({str(key): item for key, item in field_value.items()})
    return values


def _evidence_value(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    arbitration = result.get("arbitration_evidence")
    if arbitration not in (None, {}, []):
        return arbitration
    return result.get("evidence")


def canonical_evidence_fingerprint(
    result: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    target_dimensions: Sequence[str] = (),
) -> str | None:
    evidence = _evidence_value(result)
    if evidence in (None, {}, [], ""):
        return None
    targets = tuple(sorted(set(str(item) for item in target_dimensions)))
    if targets and isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
        evidence = {
            target: evidence[target]
            for target in targets
            if target in evidence
        }
        if not evidence:
            return None
    return request_fingerprint(evidence)


def canonical_result_fingerprint(
    result: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    target_dimensions: Sequence[str] = (),
) -> str:
    values = _dimension_result_values(result)
    targets = tuple(sorted(set(str(item) for item in target_dimensions)))
    if targets:
        values = {key: values[key] for key in targets if key in values}
    return request_fingerprint(
        {
            "dimension_values": values,
            "evidence": (
                {
                    key: value
                    for key, value in (
                        _evidence_value(result) or {}
                    ).items()
                    if not targets or key in targets
                }
                if isinstance(_evidence_value(result), Mapping)
                else _evidence_value(result)
            ),
            "flags": {
                field: result.get(field)
                for field in REQUIRED_STABILITY_FLAGS + HUMAN_REVIEW_FIELDS
                if field in result
            },
        }
    )


def _requires_human(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return any(result.get(field) is True for field in HUMAN_REVIEW_FIELDS)


def _adjacent_consistency(
    previous: Mapping[str, Any],
    current: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    target_dimensions: Sequence[str],
) -> tuple[bool, tuple[str, ...]]:
    previous_values = _dimension_result_values(previous)
    current_values = _dimension_result_values(current)
    targets = tuple(sorted(set(str(item) for item in target_dimensions)))
    if not targets:
        targets = tuple(sorted(set(previous_values) & set(current_values)))
    if not targets or any(
        target not in previous_values or target not in current_values
        for target in targets
    ):
        return False, ()
    conflicts = tuple(
        target
        for target in targets
        if canonical_json(previous_values[target])
        != canonical_json(current_values[target])
    )
    return not conflicts, conflicts


def _new_evidence(
    attempts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
    current_result = _attempt_result(attempts[-1])
    if current_result.get("new_evidence") is False:
        return False, "CLIENT_DECLARED_NO_NEW_EVIDENCE"
    targets = tuple(
        _dimension_values(attempts[-1].get("target_dimensions"))
    )
    current = canonical_evidence_fingerprint(
        current_result,
        target_dimensions=targets,
    )
    if current is None:
        return False, "MISSING_EVIDENCE"
    if len(attempts) == 1:
        return True, None
    current_result_fingerprint = canonical_result_fingerprint(
        current_result,
        target_dimensions=targets,
    )
    previous_results = [
        _attempt_result(attempt) for attempt in attempts[:-1]
    ]
    if any(
        canonical_result_fingerprint(
            previous_result,
            target_dimensions=targets,
        )
        == current_result_fingerprint
        for previous_result in previous_results
    ):
        return False, "DUPLICATE_RESULT"
    if any(
        previous is not None and previous == current
        for previous in (
            canonical_evidence_fingerprint(
                previous_result,
                target_dimensions=targets,
            )
            for previous_result in previous_results
        )
    ):
        return False, "DUPLICATE_EVIDENCE"
    return True, None


def _server_problem_dimensions(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    dimensions = set(problem_dimensions(result))
    confidence_by_dimension = result.get("confidence_by_dimension")
    if isinstance(confidence_by_dimension, Mapping):
        for dimension, value in confidence_by_dimension.items():
            if not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 0.7:
                dimensions.add(str(dimension))
    return tuple(sorted(dimensions))


def _stable(
    attempts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    problems: Sequence[str],
    conflicts: Sequence[str],
    consistency: bool,
) -> bool:
    result = _attempt_result(attempts[-1])
    if len(attempts) < 2 or _round(attempts[-1]) == 1:
        return False
    if not result or _requires_human(result) or problems or conflicts:
        return False
    if not all(result.get(flag) is True for flag in REQUIRED_STABILITY_FLAGS):
        return False
    if not _dimension_result_values(result):
        return False
    if canonical_evidence_fingerprint(result) is None:
        return False
    return consistency


def _round_evidence(
    attempts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    summaries = []
    for index, attempt in enumerate(attempts):
        result = _attempt_result(attempt)
        targets = tuple(
            _dimension_values(attempt.get("target_dimensions"))
        )
        consistency = False
        calculated_conflicts: tuple[str, ...] = ()
        if index > 0:
            consistency, calculated_conflicts = _adjacent_consistency(
                _attempt_result(attempts[index - 1]),
                result,
                target_dimensions=targets,
            )
        server_conflicts = tuple(
            sorted(
                set(calculated_conflicts)
                | set(conflict_dimensions(result))
            )
        )
        evidence_is_new, evidence_reason = _new_evidence(
            attempts[: index + 1]
        )
        summaries.append(
            {
                "round": _round(attempt),
                "kind": attempt.get("kind") or ROUND_KIND[_round(attempt)],
                "target_dimensions": list(
                    _dimension_values(attempt.get("target_dimensions"))
                ),
                "problem_dimensions": list(
                    _server_problem_dimensions(result)
                ),
                "conflict_dimensions": list(server_conflicts),
                "server_consistency": consistency,
                "server_new_evidence": evidence_is_new,
                "server_evidence_reason": evidence_reason,
                "evidence_fingerprint": (
                    canonical_evidence_fingerprint(
                        result,
                        target_dimensions=targets,
                    )
                ),
                "result_fingerprint": canonical_result_fingerprint(
                    result,
                    target_dimensions=targets,
                ),
                "evidence": result.get("evidence", {}),
                "arbitration_evidence": result.get(
                    "arbitration_evidence", {}
                ),
                "suggested_values": result.get("suggested_values", {}),
            }
        )
    return {"rounds": summaries}


def _stop(
    *,
    status: str,
    reason: str,
    attempts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> LoopDecision:
    return LoopDecision(
        status=status,
        machine_converged=status == "machine_converged",
        needs_human=status == "needs_human",
        reason_codes=(reason,),
        evidence=_round_evidence(attempts),
    )


def decide_next_step(
    attempts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> LoopDecision:
    """Return the only valid next transition for completed business rounds.

    The function has no I/O or clock dependency. Callers persist the returned
    decision and create a ``waiting_result`` attempt instead of pretending a
    model was invoked.
    """
    if not attempts:
        raise LoopContractError("至少需要一个已完成轮次")
    ordered = sorted(attempts, key=_round)
    rounds = [_round(attempt) for attempt in ordered]
    if rounds != list(range(1, len(ordered) + 1)):
        raise LoopContractError("轮次必须从 1 开始且连续，禁止越序")
    if len(ordered) > MAX_BUSINESS_ROUNDS:
        raise LoopContractError("最多三轮，禁止第四轮")

    current = ordered[-1]
    current_round = _round(current)
    result = _attempt_result(current)
    problems = _server_problem_dimensions(result)
    consistency = False
    calculated_conflicts: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    if len(ordered) > 1:
        consistency, calculated_conflicts = _adjacent_consistency(
            _attempt_result(ordered[-2]),
            result,
            target_dimensions=tuple(
                _dimension_values(current.get("target_dimensions"))
            ),
        )
    # Client-declared conflicts may only make the decision more conservative.
    # They can never establish consistency or convergence.
    conflicts = tuple(
        sorted(set(calculated_conflicts) | set(conflict_dimensions(result)))
    )

    if _requires_human(result):
        return _stop(
            status="needs_human",
            reason="FORCE_HUMAN",
            attempts=ordered,
        )

    has_new_evidence, evidence_reason = _new_evidence(ordered)
    if not has_new_evidence:
        return _stop(
            status="needs_human",
            reason=(
                "NO_NEW_EVIDENCE"
                if evidence_reason == "CLIENT_DECLARED_NO_NEW_EVIDENCE"
                else evidence_reason or "NO_NEW_EVIDENCE"
            ),
            attempts=ordered,
        )

    if _stable(
        ordered,
        problems=problems,
        conflicts=conflicts,
        consistency=consistency,
    ):
        reason = {
            1: "ROUND1_STABLE",
            2: "ROUND2_RESOLVED",
            3: "ROUND3_RESOLVED",
        }[current_round]
        return _stop(
            status="machine_converged",
            reason=reason,
            attempts=ordered,
        )

    if current_round == 1:
        targets = problems
        if not targets:
            return _stop(
                status="needs_human",
                reason="UNLOCATABLE_UNSTABLE_RESULT",
                attempts=ordered,
            )
        return LoopDecision(
            status="waiting_result",
            machine_converged=False,
            needs_human=False,
            reason_codes=("TARGETED_RECHECK_REQUIRED",),
            evidence=_round_evidence(ordered),
            next_round=2,
            next_kind=ROUND_KIND[2],
            target_dimensions=targets,
        )

    if current_round == 2:
        if not conflicts:
            return _stop(
                status="needs_human",
                reason="UNRESOLVED_NON_CONFLICT",
                attempts=ordered,
            )
        return LoopDecision(
            status="waiting_result",
            machine_converged=False,
            needs_human=False,
            reason_codes=("ARBITRATION_REQUIRED",),
            evidence=_round_evidence(ordered),
            next_round=3,
            next_kind=ROUND_KIND[3],
            target_dimensions=conflicts,
        )

    return _stop(
        status="needs_human",
        reason="ROUND3_FORCED_HUMAN",
        attempts=ordered,
    )


def validate_submission_scope(
    *,
    business_round: int,
    expected_kind: str,
    expected_dimensions: Sequence[str],
    submitted_kind: str,
    submitted_dimensions: Sequence[str],
) -> None:
    if business_round not in ROUND_KIND:
        raise LoopContractError("最多三轮，禁止第四轮")
    if submitted_kind != expected_kind or submitted_kind != ROUND_KIND[business_round]:
        raise LoopContractError("轮次 kind 与冻结流程不一致")
    expected = tuple(sorted(set(expected_dimensions)))
    submitted = tuple(sorted(set(submitted_dimensions)))
    if business_round == 1:
        if submitted:
            raise LoopContractError("round1 是基础判定，不能指定局部维度")
        return
    if not submitted or any(
        dimension.lower() in {"*", "all", "__all__", "full"}
        for dimension in submitted
    ):
        raise LoopContractError("round2/round3 必须只提交冻结的问题维度")
    if submitted != expected:
        raise LoopContractError("目标维度与上一轮冻结的问题维度不一致")


def validate_result_scope(
    *,
    business_round: int,
    target_dimensions: Sequence[str],
    normalized_result: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    if business_round == 1:
        return
    allowed = {str(item) for item in target_dimensions}
    unexpected_fields = sorted(
        set(normalized_result)
        - TARGETED_RESULT_METADATA_FIELDS
        - TARGETED_DIMENSION_FIELDS
    )
    forbidden = sorted(set(normalized_result) & FORBIDDEN_FULL_RESULT_FIELDS)
    if forbidden or unexpected_fields:
        fields = forbidden or unexpected_fields
        raise LoopContractError(
            "专项复判结果包含未允许字段：" + ",".join(fields)
        )

    reported = set(problem_dimensions(normalized_result))
    dimension_fields = {
        "dimension_values",
        "suggested_values",
        "confidence_by_dimension",
        "evidence",
        "arbitration_evidence",
    }
    for field in dimension_fields:
        value = normalized_result.get(field)
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            reported.update(str(key) for key in value)
        elif value not in (None, {}, []):
            raise LoopContractError(f"{field} 必须是按目标维度索引的对象")
    conflicts = normalized_result.get("conflicts")
    if conflicts not in (None, []):
        if not isinstance(conflicts, Sequence) or isinstance(
            conflicts, (str, bytes)
        ):
            raise LoopContractError("conflicts 必须是冲突对象列表")
        reported.update(_dimension_values(conflicts))
    unexpected = sorted(reported - allowed)
    if unexpected:
        raise LoopContractError(
            "专项复判结果包含未冻结维度：" + ",".join(unexpected)
        )
    values = _dimension_result_values(normalized_result)
    if not values or set(values) != allowed:
        raise LoopContractError("专项复判必须且只能返回全部目标维度值")
    if business_round == 2:
        if normalized_result.get("suggested_values") not in (None, {}):
            raise LoopContractError("round2 只能返回 dimension_values")
        if not isinstance(
            normalized_result.get("dimension_values"), Mapping
        ):
            raise LoopContractError("round2 必须返回 dimension_values")
        evidence = normalized_result.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(evidence, Mapping) or not evidence:
            raise LoopContractError("round2 必须返回按目标维度索引的新证据")
        if normalized_result.get("arbitration_evidence") not in (None, {}):
            raise LoopContractError("round2 不能夹带仲裁证据")
    else:
        if normalized_result.get("dimension_values") not in (None, {}):
            raise LoopContractError("round3 只能返回 suggested_values")
        if not isinstance(
            normalized_result.get("suggested_values"), Mapping
        ):
            raise LoopContractError("round3 必须返回 suggested_values")
        arbitration = normalized_result.get("arbitration_evidence")
        if not isinstance(arbitration, Mapping) or not arbitration:
            raise LoopContractError("round3 必须返回按冲突维度索引的仲裁证据")
        if normalized_result.get("evidence") not in (None, {}):
            raise LoopContractError("round3 只允许仲裁证据")


def normalize_targeted_model_result(
    raw_result: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    business_round: int,
    target_dimensions: Sequence[str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Convert a targeted provider response into server-owned loop metadata."""
    targets = tuple(sorted(set(str(item) for item in target_dimensions)))
    raw_values = raw_result.get("dimension_values")
    if not isinstance(raw_values, Mapping):
        raw_values = raw_result.get("suggested_values")
    if not isinstance(raw_values, Mapping):
        raw_values = {
            target: raw_result[target]
            for target in targets
            if target in raw_result
        }
    values = {
        target: raw_values[target]
        for target in targets
        if target in raw_values
    }
    evidence_field = (
        "arbitration_evidence" if business_round == 3 else "evidence"
    )
    raw_evidence = raw_result.get(evidence_field)
    if not isinstance(raw_evidence, Mapping):
        raw_evidence = {}
    evidence = {
        target: raw_evidence[target]
        for target in targets
        if target in raw_evidence
    }
    raw_confidence = raw_result.get("confidence_by_dimension")
    confidence_by_dimension = (
        {
            target: raw_confidence[target]
            for target in targets
            if target in raw_confidence
            and isinstance(raw_confidence[target], (int, float))
            and not isinstance(raw_confidence[target], bool)
        }
        if isinstance(raw_confidence, Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    schema_valid = set(values) == set(targets) and set(evidence) == set(targets)
    enum_valid = schema_valid and all(
        value is not None and not isinstance(value, (bytes, bytearray))
        for value in values.values()
    )
    cross_field_valid = schema_valid and all(
        evidence.get(target) not in (None, "", [], {})
        for target in targets
    )
    confidence_threshold_met = (
        set(confidence_by_dimension) == set(targets)
        and all(value >= 0.7 for value in confidence_by_dimension.values())
    )
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {
        (
            "suggested_values"
            if business_round == 3
            else "dimension_values"
        ): values,
        evidence_field: evidence,
        "confidence_by_dimension": confidence_by_dimension,
        "schema_valid": schema_valid,
        "enum_valid": enum_valid,
        "cross_field_valid": cross_field_valid,
        "confidence_threshold_met": confidence_threshold_met,
        "version_threshold_met": True,
        "freeze_threshold_met": True,
    }
    if raw_result.get("new_evidence") is False:
        normalized["new_evidence"] = False
    for field in HUMAN_REVIEW_FIELDS:
        if raw_result.get(field) is True:
